  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自然语言到MLIR代码生成（NL-to-MLIR）；场景：ML编译器基础设施（TensorFlow, JAX/StableHLO, PyTorch Inductor, IREE）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: SmolLM2-1.7B（小语言模型），以及CodeLlama-34B, Granite-Code-34B, StarCoder2-15B等大语言模型作为基线。核心创新不在模型架构，而在推理时约束栈（三层schema-derived constraint stack）。

Lightweight method: 本文涉及轻量化的思路：使用小模型（1.7B参数）通过结构先验约束替代梯度微调，在特定任务上达到大模型（15B-34B）的性能，且推理速度快8-25倍。但这属于代码生成领域的模型压缩/加速思路，与遥感目标检测的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提到小模型推理速度比大模型快8-25倍（per-generation speed），但未给出具体延迟、参数量（除模型规模外）、FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MLIR-Spec-150, Linalg-Spec-30, StableHLO-Spec-30, StableHLO-Held-Out-200（共410个NL-to-MLIR对），外加25个out-of-grammar stress set和30个hand-authored functional reference set。评价指标：verify-valid（验证通过率）。

Experiments: 在linalg方言上，SmolLM2-1.7B达到80.0% verify-valid（三种子平均，n=125），超过CodeLlama-34B、Granite-Code-34B、StarCoder2-15B 21-44个百分点（置信区间不重叠）。在arith+func和StableHLO-Held-Out-200上，小模型与大模型持平或略逊。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 方法仅适用于verifier语义由结构约束主导的方言，对属性值语义的方言效果有限；3. 仅验证了代码生成任务，未涉及任何视觉或遥感任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：1. "小模型+结构先验约束替代梯度微调"的思路，可类比思考在遥感目标检测中是否能用规则/知识约束替代部分模型微调；2. 推理时约束栈（rejection sampling）的思路，在星上部署中可考虑用轻量规则校验替代部分模型推理。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) underlies modern ML compiler infrastructure (TensorFlow, JAX/StableHLO, PyTorch Inductor, IREE), yet appears only in trace amounts in code-LM pretraining corpora. MLIR is also extensible by design: new dialects ship per application domain, so a fine-tuned model per dialect does not scale. We ask whether inference-time priors derived mechanically from each dialect's Operation Definition Specification (ODS) can substitute for gradient-based adaptation. First, we release four natural-language-to-MLIR benchmarks across three dialects - MLIR-Spec-150, Linalg-Spec-30, StableHLO-Spec-30, and StableHLO-Held-Out-200 - totaling 410 in-scope NL-to-MLIR pairs, plus a 25-program out-of-grammar stress set and a hand-authored n=30 functional reference set, shipped under Apache-2.0 with Gebru datasheets and Croissant 1.0 metadata. Second, we build a three-layer schema-derived constraint stack: a CFG over op signatures(C1), type-domain splits from an ODS-extracted type lattice (C2), and an SSA-scope validator driving five-retry rejection sampling (C3). Porting from arith+func+memref+linalg to StableHLO required no new constraint-layer code. On dialects whose verifier semantics are dominated by structural constraints, schema-derived priors let SmolLM2-1.7B match or exceed 15B-34B code LMs at 8-25x the per-generation speed: on linalg, SmolLM2 reaches 80.0% verify-valid (three-seed mean, n=125), beating CodeLlama-34B, Granite-Code-34B, and StarCoder2-15B by 21-44 percentage points with non-overlapping CIs. On arith+func and on the templated parametric StableHLO-Held-Out-200, where verifier semantics turn on attribute values rather than structure, the same baselines match or beat the SLM; we scope these as non-win cells. We release benchmarks, decoder, all per-prompt generations, and a reproducibility Docker image.

</details>


### [101] [Semantic Cooperative Games for Contribution Attribution in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18255)
*Pengyi Jiang,Xiaoguang Zhu,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出语义合作博弈框架(SCG)和语义Shapley值(SSV)，用于LLM多智能体系统的贡献归因，无需反事实重跑即可高效计算。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦LLM多智能体系统的贡献归因问题，属于自然语言处理/多智能体协作领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多智能体贡献归因；应用场景：LLM-based multi-agent systems；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Semantic Cooperative Games (SCG)框架，基于semantic generation hypergraph的语义值函数，Semantic Shapley Value (SSV)归因算法，SLIC单轨迹计算算法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法。SLIC算法减少了计算开销（降低93.3%计算成本），但这是归因算法的效率提升，而非模型压缩/加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 医疗基准数据集(medical benchmark)；评价指标：计算成本降低比例(93.3%)、与Monte Carlo Shapley基线的一致性、perturbation-induced score-drop profiles。

Experiments: 在满足标准集合可观测条件的医疗基准上，SLIC相比Monte Carlo Shapley基线降低93.3%计算成本且保持高度一致性；在更通用的多角色工作流中，SSV与扰动诱导的分数下降曲线对齐。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究LLM多智能体归因问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Contribution attribution has become a central problem in LLM-based multi-agent systems, where final outputs are produced through multiple agents, message exchanges, and ordered workflow dependencies. Existing attribution methods often rely on counterfactual valuation, such as removing agents or comparing score changes across altered agent subsets. In language-mediated workflows, these methods require repeated model calls, introduce high variance, and do not explicitly capture the intermediate semantic states through which agents produce, preserve, and transform task-relevant information. We propose Semantic Cooperative Games (SCG), a framework that represents a realized language flow as a semantic generation hypergraph and induces an agent-level semantic value function on this structure. We define the Semantic Shapley Value (SSV) to allocate contribution over semantic support logic, and introduce SLIC, a single-trajectory algorithm that constructs the semantic hypergraph, recovers minimal semantic supports, applies Boolean absorption, and computes SSV without rerunning agent subsets. We prove that SSV reduces to the classical Shapley value under standard set-based, fully observable, and no-order-dependence conditions. On a medical benchmark satisfying these conditions, SLIC reduces computation cost by 93.3% while remaining highly consistent with a Monte Carlo Shapley baseline. In more general multi-role workflows, SSV aligns with perturbation-induced score-drop profiles and exposes cases where semantic contribution and failure impact diverge. Overall, SLIC provides a fast, counterfactual-free, and interpretable attribution method for complex LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [102] [ProbSPARQL: Querying Knowledge Graphs with Multi-dimensional, Uncertain Numeric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18262)
*Jingcheng Wu,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Daniel Hernandez,Hongkuan Zhou,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProbSPARQL，一种扩展SPARQL以支持不确定数值测量数据（如传感器数据）概率查询的引擎，面向循环工厂知识图谱基础设施。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于知识图谱查询语言（SPARQL扩展）对不确定数值数据的概率建模与查询，属于语义网/数据库领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感场景。任务为知识图谱中不确定数值数据的概率查询，场景为循环工厂（Circular Factory）中回收产品的传感器测量数据集成。不涉及目标检测、遥感场景理解。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型架构。提出ProbSPARQL作为SPARQL的向上兼容扩展，将不确定数值建模为随机变量，分布由概率RDF字面量数据类型编码，支持分布感知表达式、概率过滤器和基于散度的连接操作。实现基于Apache Jena ARQ和Fuseki。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用项目衍生的测量数据片段（GMM编码的不确定性、基于直方图的经验粗糙度分布），以及受控本体一致性基准（最多5000个角磨机实例、150万三元组）。评价指标为查询执行可行性、过滤器下推加速比、延迟-精度权衡。

Experiments: 实验表明引擎内执行可行，过滤器下推相比应用层后处理有加速，不同散度连接决策策略存在延迟-精度权衡。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。论文本身是数据库查询语言扩展的早期试点，未涉及图像处理、深度学习推理或嵌入式部署。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于语义网/知识图谱查询领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交叉点。

Abstract: The SFB 1574 Circular Factory is building a shared knowledge graph infrastructure for integrating data about returned products. A central challenge is that circular-factory data include numeric measurements that (i) originate from sensors or are derived from sensor-based measurements, (ii) are frequently multi-dimensional, and (iii) are inherently uncertain, while downstream triage, validation, reliability-modeling, and reassembly-planning modules require queryable uncertainty representations. Current RDF and SPARQL technologies lack native support for harmonized querying and analysis of such uncertain numeric measurement data. To address this gap, we present ProbSPARQL, an upward-compatible SPARQL extension developed as an early-stage query-layer pilot for this infrastructure. ProbSPARQL models uncertain numeric values as random variables whose distributions are encoded by probabilistic RDF literal datatypes, and supports distribution-aware expressions, probabilistic filters, and divergence-based joins. We implement ProbSPARQL on Apache Jena ARQ and expose it through a Fuseki-compatible execution layer. We assess real-data applicability using project-derived measurement fragments covering GMM-encoded uncertainty and histogram-based empirical roughness distributions, and evaluate scalability separately on controlled ontology-conformant benchmarks with up to 5,000 angle-grinder instances and 1.5M triples. The results show feasible in-engine execution, filter-pushdown speedups over application-layer post-processing, and latency-accuracy trade-offs among divergence-join decision strategies.

</details>


### [103] [Position: AI/ML Deepfake Research is Misaligned with AI-Generated Non-Consensual Intimate Imagery (AIG-NCII)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18263)
*Li Qiwei,Wells Lucas Santo,Sarita Schoenebeck,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于AI生成非自愿亲密图像（AIG-NCII）的立场论文，批评现有deepfake研究过度关注真实性检测而忽视对受害者的尊严伤害，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文讨论的是AI生成图像的伦理与安全问题（deepfake/AIG-NCII），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署中的任何一个方向。属于AI伦理/社会影响类论文。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未提出任何模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未使用任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为立场论文（position paper），无实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI伦理与社会影响领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化的任何技术方法。

Abstract: AI-generated non-consensual intimate imagery (AIG-NCII) is not adequately addressed in AI/ML literature regarding AI-generated media, commonly referred to as "deepfakes". While research on deepfakes currently focuses on its epistemic harms -- or harms relating to truth and authenticity -- this is misaligned with the dominant reality of generative AI abuse involving sexualized imagery. We conduct a landscape analysis of highly-cited works to demonstrate that technical interventions addressing deepfakes almost entirely ignore AIG-NCII, limiting the research ecosystem to authenticity detection tools. In this position paper, we argue that existing interventions address viewer-centric epistemic harms, such as fraud or scams, but ignore subject-centric dignity harms, such as AIG-NCII. We illustrate that knowing an image is synthetic does not mitigate harms to subjects and may, in some cases, even exacerbate them. We conclude by offering recommendations to realign the field, including updating threat models to consider subject-centric harms and addressing AIG-NCII in AI safety research. Finally, we caution that researchers should only engage in this high-risk domain if they implement safety guardrails for both subjects and researchers and establish partnerships with domain experts in sexual violence prevention.

</details>


### [104] [Structured Synthetic Reasoning Data for Arithmetic Fine-Tuning of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18266)
*Jake O'Grady,Effirul Ramlan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过GPT-5-mini生成结构化合成推理数据，用LoRA微调小语言模型（Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B），提升其多步算术推理能力，在消费级硬件上实现部署。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦小语言模型的算术推理能力提升，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然涉及"small language models"和"consumer-hardware constraints"，但这是NLP领域的轻量部署，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：算术推理（arithmetic reasoning）；应用场景：消费级硬件上的小语言模型本地部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B小语言模型，使用LoRA进行参数高效微调。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。仅涉及LoRA微调（参数高效微调）和消费级硬件部署（Apple M4, 16 GB RAM），但这不是轻量化方法本身。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及在Apple M4, 16 GB RAM的消费级硬件上运行，但未给出具体推理速度或资源占用数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GSM8K（基础数据集，生成21,250条变体）、MultiArith、SVAMP；评价指标：Exact-match accuracy（精确匹配准确率）。

Experiments: Qwen3-0.6B: GSM8K准确率从36.5%提升至49.1%；Qwen3-1.7B: GSM8K从53.5%提升至66.5%，MultiArith达98.9%，SVAMP达73.0%。消融实验：定性分析显示微调后模型推理轨迹更短，算术错误和干扰项错误减少，self-consistency采样收益更一致。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何视觉任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化核心方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；4. 仅针对算术推理这一NLP任务，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1. 合成数据增强思路——在遥感目标检测中也可考虑用生成模型合成带旋转框标注的遥感图像变体（不同分辨率、不同光照、不同传感器噪声）来提升小模型检测能力；2. LoRA微调思路——如果未来在轨部署涉及大语言模型与视觉模型的融合（如视觉-语言模型用于遥感场景理解），LoRA可作为在轨模型更新的轻量手段。但整体而言，该论文对"遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive for local deployment, but they often struggle with multi-step arithmetic reasoning. We study whether structured synthetic reasoning data can improve this behaviour under consumer-hardware constraints. Starting from GSM8K, we generated a 21,250-example corpus of grade-school arithmetic word-problem variants using GPT-5-mini, combining natural-language solution traces, light Socratic-style cues, structural variation, and irrelevant distractor context. We then fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B with LoRA on consumer hardware (Apple M4, 16 GB RAM). Exact-match accuracy on GSM8K improved from 36.5% to 49.1% for Qwen3-0.6B and from 53.5% to 66.5% for Qwen3-1.7B. For Qwen3-1.7B, transfer to related arithmetic benchmarks was stronger, reaching 98.9% on MultiArith and 73.0% on SVAMP, compared with 54.4% and 45.3% for the base model. Qualitative analysis suggests that fine-tuned models produce shorter reasoning traces, make fewer arithmetic and distractor-use errors, and benefit more consistently from self-consistency sampling. These results show that low-cost synthetic data design can materially improve arithmetic adaptation in small language models. Because the intervention combines Socratic-style cues with other data-design choices, we interpret the gains as evidence for structured synthetic reasoning data rather than as a causal test of Socratic guidance alone.

</details>


### [105] [Fence: Specialized SLM Guardrails for LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18268)
*Kumud Lakara,Ruibo Shi,Fran Silavong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出使用基于合成数据训练的小语言模型(SLM)作为大语言模型(LLM)应用的专业护栏，并引入类似GAN的合成数据生成方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM应用的安全护栏（guardrails）设计，涉及语言模型的内容过滤、幻觉检测、话题漂移等问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM应用的安全护栏（guardrails），包括毒性/偏见过滤、幻觉检测、话题漂移、行为偏差检测。场景：自然语言处理/大模型应用安全。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 使用小语言模型(SLM)作为护栏模型；合成数据生成方法受GAN设计启发。具体架构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 论文中"小语言模型(SLM)"相对于LLM可视为一种轻量化思路，但这是语言模型领域的轻量化，与遥感目标检测的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。不涉及任何遥感或视觉领域的轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成数据训练SLM。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验表明基于高质量合成数据训练的SLM护栏在性能上优于基于提示的LLM护栏。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感视觉或部署优化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：合成数据生成方法（GAN启发）可用于遥感目标检测中的数据增强，但论文本身并未涉及遥感或视觉领域。

Abstract: Real-world applications that use closed-source large language models (LLMs) need advanced safety measures that go beyond the basic content filters. Content moderation filters such as toxicity and bias have relatively standard definitions where as application specific guardrails like hallucination, topic drift and behaviour deviation are more difficult to model and can vary by use case. Additionally, data scarcity and annotation costs, make the process of creating and testing specialized guardrails challenging. In this work, we propose using Small Language Models (SLMs) trained on synthetic data as specialized guardrails for LLM applications. We introduce a novel synthetic data generation method inspired by the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) to generate high quality synthetic data samples which can be used to train SLMs to encode use case specific guardrail information and hence function as specialized guardrails. Our experiments demonstrate that SLM guardrails trained on high quality synthetic data show performance gains over prompt based LLM guardrails.

</details>


### [106] [Wisdom of LLM Crowds: Aggregation and Contamination in Language Model Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18269)
*Igor Douven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究15个LLM在二元预测问题上的"群体智慧"聚合效应，发现线性聚合优于个体模型，并揭示了训练数据污染对评估的严重影响。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究对象是LLM的群体智慧聚合，属于自然语言处理/机器学习基础研究，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、星上部署或任何轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为LLM概率预测聚合，场景为预测市场（prediction market），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Model architecture: 使用15个LLM作为基模型，聚合方法包括经典方法（未详述）和学习的聚合器（多层感知机MLP、逻辑回归Logistic Regression），以及符号回归（Symbolic Regression）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 254个二元预测市场问题（binary prediction market questions），评价指标未明确说明（提及准确率差距35.8% vs 8.9%）。

Experiments: 学习聚合器（MLP和逻辑回归）优于所有个体模型和经典聚合方法；逻辑回归与MLP性能相当；符号回归发现模型分歧信号（model-disagreement signal）是最低复杂度的有用公式；训练截止污染（training cutoff contamination）显著影响评估结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署的任何方面。

Abstract: The wisdom of crowds -- the finding that aggregating judgments across individuals often outperforms the best individual -- has been extensively studied with human forecasters. Whether the same phenomenon emerges when the ``crowd'' consists of large language models (LLMs) is an open question with both theoretical and practical implications. We elicited probability estimates from 15 LLMs on 254 binary prediction market questions and evaluated classical and learned aggregation methods. Learned aggregators -- a multilayer perceptron and a logistic regression -- outperformed all individual models and classical methods. The logistic regression was found to match the neural network, suggesting that the benefit of learned aggregation derives from learning a linear combination of diverse model outputs rather than from nonlinear interactions. Symbolic regression applied to the neural network's learned mapping recovered a pure model-disagreement signal as the lowest-complexity useful formula on the Pareto frontier, further supporting this interpretation. Training cutoff contamination proved a pervasive confound: the apparent capability gap between frontier cloud models and smaller local models collapsed from 35.8% to 8.9% on a clean subset of questions resolving after all models' training cutoffs, and individual model rankings showed only moderate stability. Even when the prediction market is evaluated at each model's training cutoff, LLMs remained substantially less accurate, indicating a genuine gap in collective information aggregation. These findings suggest that LLM crowds can exhibit wisdom-of-crowds effects, but that contamination-free evaluation is essential for reliable assessment.

</details>


### [107] [Trajectory-Aware Clinical Risk Prediction via Severity-Grounded Knowledge Graphs and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18270)
*Kyunghoon Jeon,Youmin Ko,Woohwan Jung,Hyunjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个名为TRACER的临床风险预测框架，通过医学知识图谱、非结构化临床笔记和相似病例增强患者表征，用于死亡率预测和再入院预测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是医疗健康领域的EHR（电子健康记录）临床风险预测，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：临床风险预测（死亡率预测、再入院预测）；场景：医疗健康/电子健康记录；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: TRACER框架包含四个组件：(1) 医学知识图谱构建（含严重程度信息）；(2) 从知识图谱检索临床相关的严重程度加权路径；(3) 从非结构化临床笔记中提取临床相关事件；(4) 用相似病例增强患者上下文。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT相关内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV；评价指标：Macro F1（死亡率预测任务提升28.5%，再入院预测任务提升19.7%）。

Experiments: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上对比SOTA基线，死亡率预测Macro F1提升28.5%，再入院预测提升19.7%。摘要未说明消融实验、参数量对比、推理速度等。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感目标检测或轻量化方面的局限性信息。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理效率、是否可迁移至遥感领域。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：知识图谱增强表征的思路或许可类比到遥感场景中引入地理知识图谱来辅助目标检测，但与本课题核心（轻量化在轨检测）关联甚微。

Abstract: While Electronic Health Records (EHRs) offer a wealth of clinical data, effectively augmenting a patient's records with heterogeneous external knowledge to predict the patient's clinical risk remains a significant challenge. Existing methods fail to capture disease severity, treatment responses, and nuanced clinical progression, due to data sparsity and the underutilization of unstructured clinical notes. To address these challenges, we propose TRACER (a trajectory-aware and clinically grounded prediction framework) that (1) constructs a medical knowledge graph enriched with severity information from medical literature, (2) retrieves clinically relevant, severity-weighted paths of a patient's progression from the knowledge graph, (3) extracts clinically relevant events from unstructured clinical notes, and (4) augments patient context with similar peer cases. Experiments on the MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets demonstrate large gains over state-of-the-art baselines, with up to 28.5% increase in Macro F1 score for the mortality prediction task, and 19.7% increase for the readmission prediction task.

</details>


### [108] [Using LLMs for Explainable, Data-Driven Insight Generation from Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18271)
*Ria Mundhra,Gustavo Sato dos Santos,Michael Benedikt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向时间序列预测的可解释性框架，利用大语言模型生成基于结构化证据的自然语言解释，并在金融和货运定价场景验证效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦时间序列预测的可解释性（explainable AI for time series forecasting），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。应用场景为金融指数预测和货运定价，与遥感在轨智能无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时间序列预测的可解释自然语言生成。场景：金融预测（NASDAQ-100指数）、货运定价（Vortexa数据）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 三组件框架：(i) 结构化解释因子提取，(ii) 基于证据的解释生成，(iii) 可扩展评估（可读性、逻辑一致性、说服力）。使用大语言模型（LLM）但未说明具体模型名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NASDAQ-100指数历史数据、Vortexa货运定价数据。评价指标：可读性、逻辑一致性、说服力（均为人工/自动评估，非标准检测指标）。

Experiments: 在两个案例上验证，生成解释在可读性、一致性和说服力上接近分析师撰写的解释。无消融实验或对比方法说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无可借鉴的模型结构或压缩方法。仅可解释性框架的设计思路（证据约束生成）可能对遥感领域可解释AI有间接参考价值，但非本课题核心方向。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果硬要关联，其"evidence-conditioned generation"（基于证据约束的生成）思路可用于遥感目标检测结果的可解释性报告生成，但这属于下游应用而非检测模型本身。

Abstract: Time series forecasts are widely used in decision-critical domains, where they are rarely consumed without accompanying explanations. Producing such explanations is usually a manual and costly process, and attempts to automate it using large language models often suffer from hallucination when applied to temporal data. We propose a domain-agnostic framework for grounded natural language explanation generation for time series forecasts, illustrated in Figure 1. The framework consists of three components: (i) extraction of structured explanatory factors from historical analyst-written explanations, (ii) evidence-conditioned explanation generation, and (iii) scalable evaluation for readability, logical consistency, and persuasiveness. The design explicitly constrains generation to verifiable evidence, reducing unsupported claims.
  We evaluate the framework on a financial forecasting case study involving the NASDAQ-100 index and a freight pricing case study using data from Vortexa. Results show that generated explanations approached analyst-written explanations in terms of readability, consistency and persuasiveness. These findings demonstrate that grounded explanation generation for time series forecasting can be achieved at scale without domain-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [109] [Deep Reinforcement Learning to Master the Asymmetric Strategy of Baghchal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18296)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Aaryan Shakya,Manoj Shakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了四种深度强化学习算法（DQN、REINFORCE、PPO、MuZero）在尼泊尔棋盘游戏Baghchal上的表现，发现MuZero胜率最高，PPO在计算成本与性能间取得较好平衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是Baghchal棋盘游戏，属于深度强化学习在博弈领域的应用，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：棋盘游戏博弈（Baghchal）；场景：非遥感、非卫星、非视觉感知；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：老虎与山羊（游戏棋子）。

Model architecture: DQN（Deep Q-Network）、REINFORCE（策略梯度）、PPO（Proximal Policy Optimization）、MuZero（基于模型的强化学习，结合MCTS蒙特卡洛树搜索）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及PPO相比MuZero具有"significantly reduced computational costs"，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：不适用（使用Baghchal游戏环境）；评价指标：胜率（win rate）、平局率（draw rate）、平均捕获数（average captures）、训练收敛性（training convergence）、计算成本（computational cost）。

Experiments: MuZero在老虎角色上胜率86%，山羊角色上胜率62%，表现最佳；PPO在计算成本与性能间取得较好平衡；DQN偏向老虎角色；REINFORCE表现较弱。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节或方法论。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然强化学习方法（如PPO、MuZero）在决策规划上有优势，但本文不涉及视觉感知、遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Baghchal is a two-player asymmetric board game with Nepali origins where four tigers are to capture goats and twenty goats desire to keep tigers in immobility. Although Baghchal has a complex structure which is strategic, has perfect information structure, and has cultural meaning, it has not been adequately covered in deep reinforcement learning (RL) literature. This paper gives a systematic exploration of four deep RL solutions Deep Q-Network (DQN), REINFORCE, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and MuZero that are trained on one side of the asymmetric gameplay of Baghchal and then evaluated on the other side. The algorithms are rated based on win rate, draw rate, average captures, training convergence and computational cost. It is experimentally found that MuZero generates the best performance in both tasks, achieving 86 percent win over these Tiger and 62 percent win over these Goat and the ability to do so is due to the model-based planning machine through the Monte Carlo Tree Search. PPO is the most realistic algorithm and is provided to be competitive over both asymmetric tasks with significantly reduced computational costs compared to MuZero. Emergent strategic behavior analysis shows that model-based strategies are optimal over long-horizon planning, whereas value-based counterparts like DQN are more biased up towards the Tiger role owing to the more substantial reward signal.

</details>


### [110] [MAGE: Human-Like Macro Placement via Agentic Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18536)
*Andrew B. Kahng,Sayak Kundu,Bodhisatta Pramanik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态多智能体框架MAGE，用于芯片宏布局的自动化精调，通过自然语言编码专家知识而非从数据中学习。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是芯片物理设计中的宏布局（macro placement）问题，属于电子设计自动化（EDA）领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 芯片宏布局精调（macro placement refinement），属于集成电路物理设计流程，非遥感场景。

Model architecture: MAGE（Macro Placement Agentic Engine），多模态多智能体框架，包含六阶段工作流（结构化布局规则、视觉检查、迭代精调），锦标赛式精调模式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向EDA工具链，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NanGate45和GlobalFoundries 12nm工艺的9个设计。评价指标：WNS（最差负时序裕量）、TNS（总负时序裕量）、线长、功耗，以及提出的四个人类相似度指标（notch score, whitespace score, pocket score, alignment score）。

Experiments: 在9个设计上，MAGE相比商业宏布局器在WNS上提升11.1%-19.3%，TNS上提升70.0%-74.0%。在3个NanGate45设计上，相比人类专家提升WNS 18.3%、TNS 72.5%；相比Hier-RTLMP提升WNS 47.0%、TNS 80.4%。人类相似度指标提升6%-48%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于EDA/芯片设计领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉点。

Abstract: Macro placement still requires substantial manual refinement in industrial physical design flows. We present MAGE (Macro Placement Agentic Engine), a multimodal multi-agent framework for macro placement refinement. MAGE decomposes the macro placement task into a six-phase workflow that combines structured floorplanning rules, visual checks, and iterative refinement. Expert floorplanning knowledge is encoded through natural-language directives and validation criteria, rather than learned from labeled placement data. A tournament-style refinement mode evaluates multiple candidate placements and propagates feedback from higher-quality solutions. We also introduce four metrics for quantifying human-likeness in macro placement: notch score, whitespace score, pocket score, and alignment score. These metrics capture structural properties used by expert designers but not directly measured by conventional PPA metrics. Across nine designs in NanGate45 and GlobalFoundries 12nm enablements, MAGE achieves geometric-mean improvements of 11.1%-19.3% in WNS and 70.0%-74.0% in TNS over commercial macro placers. On the three NanGate45 designs, for which human-expert and Hier-RTLMP baselines are available, MAGE improves WNS and TNS by 18.3% and 72.5% over the human expert, and by 47.0% and 80.4% over Hier-RTLMP, with comparable wirelength and power. On human-likeness metrics, MAGE improves the overall score by 6%-48% over all baselines. Additional case studies on anonymized netlists, unseen designs, dense rectilinear floorplans, and high-utilization settings show that the framework transfers to new placement settings without design-specific retraining.

</details>


### [111] [Engineering Trustworthy Agentic AI for Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18548)
*Omar Al-Refai,Ibrahim Shahbaz,Adam Ali Husseinat,Michael Mandulak,Jaewon Kim,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一篇关于Agentic AI系统可信性（trustworthiness）的综述，从工程约束角度讨论安全、鲁棒性、可解释性、可审计性和隐私安全五个维度，并应用于电力系统、自动驾驶/机器人/UAV、高性能计算和通信网络四个工程领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是Agentic AI可信性综述，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。虽然UAV被提及为一个应用领域，但论文讨论的是AI系统的可信性验证框架，而非遥感图像目标检测或模型压缩部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Agentic AI系统可信性综述；应用场景：电力系统、自动驾驶/机器人/UAV、高性能计算、通信网络；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。论文讨论的是Agentic AI系统的架构设计模式，而非特定检测或轻量化网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是可信性验证框架，而非在轨或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。论文提出的是可信性量化指标（quantitative metrics）的概念框架，但未给出具体数值或标准数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。这是一篇综述论文，不包含实验验证。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文讨论的是Agentic AI系统的可信性工程框架，不涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在技术层面没有交集。如果非要寻找间接关联，论文中提到的"安全关键工程领域的分级认证机制（graded certification regimes）"这一概念，或许可以启发在轨部署模型的可信性评估思路，但属于非常间接的参考。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence systems, capable of autonomous perception, planning, tool use, and multi-step action, are increasingly proposed for critical engineering domains where decisions carry physical, operational, or economic consequences. This survey addresses a gap in current literature by treating trustworthiness, whether agentic behavior can be verified, audited, and trusted under the constraints that engineering practice actually requires, as a first-class engineering property, rather than evaluating agentic AI by task capability alone. The study adopts a trustworthiness model organized around five cross-cutting dimensions: safety and constraint satisfaction; robustness and reliability; transparency and interpretability; accountability and auditability; and privacy and security. This is mapped onto an agentic assurance workflow spanning perception through audit. Building on this foundation, agentic systems architectures, threats, concrete trust mechanisms, and quantitative metrics are surveyed for direct application in agentic systems development and evaluation. These principles are then examined across four constraint-bound engineering domains: power systems, autonomous vehicles/robotics/UAVs, high-performance computing, and communication networks, identifying recurring design patterns, shared failure modes, and domain-specific gaps. Synthesizing across those domains, agentic AI trustworthiness is shown to be a single problem, with a path outlined toward a reusable, cross-domain assurance framework analogous to the graded certification regimes used by mature safety-critical engineering fields.

</details>


### [112] [Attacking Graph Foundation Models Through Their Shared Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18567)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究图基础模型的alignment层在推理时对抗攻击下的脆弱性，发现该层是独立于GNN的新攻击面，且OpenGraph的谱分词器在极低预算下即可崩溃。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于图神经网络/图基础模型的对抗攻击安全性研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图基础模型alignment层的推理时对抗攻击；场景：图域泛化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 图基础模型（graph foundation model），包含alignment layer（谱分词器、文本嵌入空间、离散码本）和decoder。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 6个公开图基础模型（含spectral tokenizers, text embedding spaces, discrete codebook）；攻击成功率、representation-space perturbation budget、clean accuracy等。

Experiments: 在6个公开模型上验证alignment层脆弱性；OpenGraph的谱分词器在仅需普通GNN 1/5预算下崩溃；输入空间攻击（编辑边、特征、文本）在3/6模型上去除至少一半正确预测。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究图基础模型的安全脆弱性，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: A graph foundation model generalizes across graph domains by mapping every input into one shared representation before any task reasoning. We call this map the alignment layer, the component that separates a graph foundation model from a graph neural network, and we show it is a distinct attack surface that prior work has not studied. We attack it at inference time, with no access to training, on six public models spanning spectral tokenizers, text embedding spaces, and a discrete codebook. A directed representation-space perturbation collapses every model, but at a budget comparable to the representation norm a plain graph network also needs, with one exception: OpenGraph, whose spectral tokenizer collapses at a fifth of that budget, an alignment-specific fragility a plain network does not share and which a same-representation control traces to the tokenizer rather than the decoder. A realizable input-space attack that edits edges, features, or text removes at least half the correct predictions on three of the six models at peak. How much of this fragility an input-access attacker realizes tracks how directly the decoder reads the representation, and not the clean accuracy a task leaves; we measure this carrier gain structurally from the decoder's local Lipschitz sensitivity, and report clean-accuracy headroom as a within-model ordering heuristic that does not survive on realizable attacks.

</details>


### [113] [When Does Machine Learning Beat Value Sorting? A Three-Dataset Diagnostic of Exposure-Weighted Shipment Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18573)
*Jize Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究供应链物流中的延迟风险排序与价值排序对比，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是供应链物流领域的delay-risk模型评估，讨论的是shipment inspection优先级排序问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：延迟风险排序与价值排序对比；应用场景：供应链物流（SCMS procurement, DataCo logistics, Olist e-commerce）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。仅提及使用机器学习模型进行delay severity预测，以及cost-sensitive retraining方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SCMS procurement, DataCo logistics, Olist e-commerce三个供应链数据集；评价指标：predictive accuracy, R², calibration bias, 以及10% review budget下的percentage points (pp)差值；实验设置：leakage-controlled rolling-origin evaluation, 1000-sample paired bootstrap confidence intervals, nested-CV。

Experiments: 主要发现：排序按predicted delay severity × known value (M1)在三个数据集中均优于仅按severity排序，但总体上不优于按value排序。在10% review budget下，M1减去VALUE_ONLY的差值为SCMS: -5.5pp, DataCo: +10.1pp, Olist: -4.9pp。差异与severity learnability一致。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。论文本身聚焦于供应链管理中的模型评估协议，而非计算机视觉或深度学习模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Delay-risk models are usually judged by predictive accuracy. What matters in practice is narrower: with capacity to review only a few shipments, which ones should a manager check first? We evaluate whether machine learning clears a demanding no-model baseline: inspect the highest-value shipments first. Across three real supply-chain contexts: SCMS procurement, DataCo logistics, and Olist e-commerce, we use leakage-controlled rolling-origin evaluation and 1000-sample paired bootstrap confidence intervals. Ranking by predicted delay severity times known value (M1) beats severity-only ranking in all three datasets, yet it does not generally beat value sorting. At a 10% review budget, M1 minus VALUE_ONLY is -5.5 percentage points (pp) for SCMS, +10.1 pp for DataCo, and -4.9 pp for Olist. The divide is consistent with severity learnability: DataCo has R^2 = 0.27 and calibration bias of +0.01 days, whereas SCMS and Olist have R^2 of approximately -0.02 and negative calibration bias. Nested-CV cost-sensitive retraining does not deliver a stable improvement over M1. Rather than proposing a new learning algorithm, this paper presents a deployment diagnostic and evaluation protocol. Value sorting should remain a permanent benchmark, and ML should be deployed only after severity learnability and calibration have been audited and the model clears that gate under leakage-controlled rolling-origin evaluation.

</details>


### [114] [SciHazard: A Benchmark for Measuring Scientific Safety Risks with Decomposed Harm Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18665)
*Chunxiao Li,Yuan Xiong,Lijun Li,Tianyi Du,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向大语言模型科学安全风险的基准测试集SciHazard和评估框架DeHarm-Score，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是大语言模型（LLM）的科学安全风险评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。论文任务为LLM科学安全风险评测，涉及12个学科的有害问题与过度安全问题的分类与评估。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明目标检测模型结构。论文评估对象为31个前沿LLM和deep research agents，不涉及遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SciHazard（2400个有害问题+600个过度安全问题，覆盖12个学科）。评价指标：DeHarm-Score（分解为查询危害严重性、拒绝行为、响应级风险，其中响应级风险进一步分解为Executability和Net-new risk）。

Experiments: 专家验证研究表明DeHarm-Score相比最强基线在专家标注一致性上提升90.17%。对31个前沿LLM和deep research agents进行了基准测试，发现deep research agents的平均DeHarm-Score比标准LLM高32.3%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。论文本身聚焦LLM安全评测，局限性在于其评估框架的泛化性等，但与本研究课题无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署中的任何技术要素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support science, but they can also convert hazardous scientific knowledge into actionable misuse guidance. Existing benchmarks often rely on templated queries disconnected from real-world hazards, and employ LLM-as-a-Judge paradigms without domain grounding. To address this, we introduce SciHazard, a real-world-grounded benchmark for scientific risks and a dataset agnostic evaluation framework for measuring harmfulness. SciHazard contains 2400 hazardous questions and 600 oversafety questions across 12 disciplines, with both queries grounded in regulated entities and documented failure scenarios. To compute \textsc{DeHarm-Score} , we develop a decomposed evaluating procedure that combines query hazard severity, refusal behavior, and response-level risk. For non-refused responses, it further decomposes response-level harm into \textsc{Executability}, quantified via dynamic checklists with importance weighting, and \textsc{Net-new risk}, assessed through retrieval-augmented claim extraction and synthesis-barrier verification. An expert-validation study shows that \textsc{DeHarm-Score} improves agreement with expert annotations by 90.17\% over the strongest baseline. We benchmark 31 frontier LLMs and deep research agents in an extensive scientific safety evaluation. Notably, deep research agents yield 32.3\% higher mean \textsc{DeHarm-Score} than standard LLMs, exposing autonomous agents as a critical blind spot in current safety defenses. Code and dataset are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DeharmScore-7B55.

</details>


### [115] [Semantic Primes as Explanans for Emotion in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18691)
*Frank Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型（LLMs）中的情感机制解释，提出使用自然语义元语言（NSM）的语义基元作为比情感表征更基础的解释变量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是大语言模型（LLMs）的情感机制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM情感机制解释；应用场景：自然语言处理/认知科学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Llama-1B, Gemma-2B, Gemma-9B, OLMo-7B 四种指令微调LLM。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。虽然涉及1B/2B/7B/9B不同规模模型，但研究目的是情感解释而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提到使用"干预"（intervening）方式评估NSM基元对情感控制的效果，指标为"控制强度"（three times as strongly）和"选择性"（twice as selectively）。

Experiments: 实验表明NSM语义基元在四个LLM上可恢复，且相比基于评价（appraisal）的方向，基于基元的方向对情感控制强度约高3倍、选择性约高2倍。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/认知科学领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Progresses have been made on understanding emotion mechanisms of large language models (LLMs). However, how to explain emotion in LLMs, or even what constitutes good explanations, are less clear. Emotion representations, components, circuits are widely recoverable, but as explanations of a model's own computation they are circular; the emotion space dimensions tend to be arbitrary and non-terminating. A pressing question to ask is whether a more primitive set of internal variables does the work: the semantic primes of the Natural Semantic Metalanguage (NSM). Across four instruction-tuned LLMs (Llama-1B, Gemma-2B, Gemma-9B, OLMo-7B), experiments show that the NSM primes are (1) recoverable internal elements; and (2) on the reference model, intervening with a prime based direction controls emotion about three times as strongly, and twice as selectively, as the best appraisal based direction; and (3) the model treats a prime based explication as interchangeable with the corresponding emotion. These evidences suggest that NSM primes seem to be better explanans for emotion in LLMs than many alternative options according to scientific explanations criteria.

</details>


### [116] [Do AI-Native Biotechs Need Departments? Benchmarking Company World Models for AI-Driven Drug Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18696)
*Yinan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论AI原生生物技术公司的组织架构设计，提出"公司世界模型"概念，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。论文主题是AI-agent在生物技术公司组织架构中的应用，属于AI for Science / 商业AI领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感/卫星/目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是AI-agent组织架构（human-org-mimic, asset-centric, value-conversion架构），非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用45个回顾性公开信息决策案例作为benchmark，但这是商业决策数据集，非遥感数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文比较了不同AI-agent架构在商业决策任务上的表现，使用自动价值转换评分和盲审评估。

Limitations: 论文明确说明仅为dry-lab研究，未建立真实药物成功、临床获益或收入预测准确性。与遥感方向无关，无参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不提供任何可借鉴的技术方法、实验设计或研究思路。

Abstract: AI-native biotechnology companies are often designed by copying human biotech org charts into agent roles. We argue for a different abstraction: a Company World Model, defined as a persistent asset-to-value state representation with transition models, explicit value functions, planning, and updating across scientific, regulatory, BD, commercial, financial, and execution constraints. We introduce a dry-lab benchmark for testing whether AI-agent organizations should mimic departments or operate around such a world model. The benchmark contains 45 retrospective public-information decision cases with strict time cutoffs, hidden outcomes, common schemas, automatic scoring, and blinded pairwise judging. We compare human-org-mimic, stronger human-org-mimic-plus, AI-native asset-centric, and AI-native value-conversion architectures. The value-conversion architecture is a prompt-level approximation of a Company World Model: a Live Asset Value Record updated by Deal, Approval, Revenue, and Investment Arbiter loops. Under a success function defined by external BD, regulatory approval and launch, and revenue discipline, it achieved the highest automatic value-conversion score and was strongly preferred over the original baselines by value-specific blinded judges. Stress tests narrowed the claim: a stronger human baseline remained competitive, and a neutral judge did not show robust value-conversion dominance. Codex-only mechanistic ablations suggest that Revenue Room, Deal Room, and Approval Room carry useful work under the target objective. The central finding is objective-sensitive: departments may remain useful governance views, but the core AI-native operating primitive should be a shared, predictive asset-to-value state rather than a static human org chart. The study is dry-lab only and does not establish real-world drug success, clinical benefit, or revenue prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [117] [DWM: Separating World Effects from Actions in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18715)
*Yi-Ge Zhang,Tianqi Du,Qi Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种分解式世界模型DWM，将潜在状态转移显式拆分为动作驱动分量和动作不变（环境固有）分量，以提升模型在存在持久环境动力学下的控制性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于model-based control / world model / reinforcement learning领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：model-based control中的latent world model学习；应用场景：机器人控制/强化学习基准环境（PushT, Reacher, TwoRoom）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于latent world model架构，在原有predictor基础上增加一个auxiliary world head，通过world-contrastive objective正则化使其具有action-invariant特性，并通过orthogonality constraint将original pred head与world head解耦，实现显式加性分解。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 构建了三个W-variant基准环境：PushT-W, Reacher-W, TwoRoom-W，每个环境引入一种不同的action-invariant动态。评价指标为CEM planning success rate。

Experiments: 在flat（无额外世界动态）版本上DWM与强基线持平；在W-variant版本上，CEM planning success平均绝对提升13.1%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身局限：仅验证了控制类任务，未讨论对视觉表征或检测任务的可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：将复杂耦合信号分解为不同来源的分量（如遥感目标检测中可将场景变化分解为卫星平台运动引起的背景变化 vs. 目标本身的运动变化），但这种类比非常间接，且论文未涉及视觉检测任务。

Abstract: Latent world models underpin much of modern model-based control, yet current action-conditioned formulations supervise
  the next-latent transition with a single, undifferentiated target, forcing a monolithic learning signal to absorb every
  source of state change. In real world, however, transitions arise from two heterogeneous sources: an action-driven
  component induced by the agent, and an action-invariant world effect -- the change that would still occur under a null
  action, dictated by the environment's intrinsic dynamics (e.g., gravity-driven sliding, inertia, contact rebound, and
  persistent drift). Fusing them into a single target entangles the two inside the latent transition, prevents the model
  from attributing observed changes to their underlying causes, and undermines the transferability of the learned
  dynamics. We introduce DWM (Decomposed World Model), a supervision-level framework that operationalizes this
  decomposition. DWM augments the predictor of a latent world model with an auxiliary world head, regularized by a
  normalized world-contrastive objective to be action-invariant, while the original pred head is coupled to it via an
  orthogonality constraint; together, the two signals induce an explicit additive decomposition of the predicted
  transition into an action-invariant and a complementary action-driven component, without altering the underlying
  architecture or inference pipeline. To evaluate DWM under persistent world effects, we construct W-variants of three
  standard control benchmarks -- PushT-W, Reacher-W, and TwoRoom-W -- each instantiating a distinct action-invariant
  dynamic. DWM matches strong baselines on the flat counterparts and delivers a mean absolute improvement of 13.1% in CEM
  planning success across the W-variants.

</details>


### [118] [One Rewrite to Fix Them All? Type-Aware Repair Allocation for Text-to-Image Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18724)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Shuguang Cui,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TARA框架，将文本到图像生成中的提示优化问题建模为原子修复分配，通过类型感知的修复算子提升语义准确性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是Text-to-Image生成中的prompt优化问题，属于多模态生成/视觉语言方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：文本到图像生成（Text-to-Image generation）的prompt优化；场景：通用图像生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: TARA框架包含诊断（diagnosis）、分配（allocation）、编译（compilation）和语义修复门控（semantic repair gate）四个模块，采用类型感知的修复算子（type-conditioned repair operator）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文声称是training-free方法，但这是指无需重新训练生成器，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提到运行速度：16.0秒/提示词（局部设置下），远达不到实时或星上部署要求。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DSG、TIFA；评价指标：语义准确性（semantic accuracy）；生成器：4个frozen generators。

Experiments: 在DSG和TIFA上，TARA在全部8个benchmark-generator组合中取得最佳语义准确性，相比VisualPrompter在DSG上提升5.6分、TIFA上提升2.6分，同时保持图像质量。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、加速或部署方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于T2I生成领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术问题。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generators often fail to follow their prompts faithfully, producing wrong counts, swapped attributes, ambiguous relations, and illegible text. Prompt optimization repairs such failures by rewriting the user prompt, requiring no generator retraining, and has yielded promising results. However, existing optimizers absorb heterogeneous failures into one uniform prompt expansion, even though each calls for different repair language. We formulate semantic prompt optimization as atomic repair allocation: each failed proposition is routed to a type-conditioned repair operator before the resulting local constraints are compiled into one executable prompt. We instantiate this formulation in the training-free Type-Aware Repair Allocation (TARA) framework, which separates diagnosis, allocation, compilation, and a semantic repair gate, an accept-or-revert controller over exactly one prescribed repair that prevents semantic regressions. Extensive experiments on DSG and TIFA across four frozen generators demonstrate that TARA achieves the best semantic accuracy in all eight benchmark-generator cells, improving over VisualPrompter by 5.6 and 2.6 points on DSG and TIFA, respectively, while maintaining image quality and running fastest in our matched local setting at 16.0 seconds versus 20.0 seconds per prompt.

</details>


### [119] [Evaluating medical AI under missing information: same-provider judges and human raters change apparent safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18828)
*Koyar Afrasyab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究医疗AI在信息缺失场景下的开放性临床对话安全评估，构建了LLM裁判组与临床医生锚定的评估框架，发现裁判选择显著影响安全性排名且LLM裁判比临床医生更宽容。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医疗AI对话安全评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医疗AI开放性临床对话安全评估；场景：信息缺失下的临床对话；传感器/数据类型：文本对话；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 评估对象为四个LLM：Claude Opus 4.8、GPT-5.5、Grok 4.3、Gemini 3.5 Flash；评估框架为四提供商的LLM裁判组+盲法临床医生锚定参考。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息。参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等均未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：HealthBench对话数据集（截断后一半用户轮次）；评价指标：Fleiss' kappa（裁判间一致性）、逻辑回归（同提供商偏差）、MedQA准确率（封闭式锚定）；人工标注协议已公开。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 裁判选择显著改变模型安全性排名，裁判间一致性仅中等（kappa=0.65），存在同提供商偏差；(2) LLM裁判比临床医生更宽容（66-84% vs 52%），在临床不确定子集上差距更大；(3) MedQA锚定确认模型准确率高且选项顺序效应在±5分等价区间内，安全差距源于校准而非知识。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提取任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于医疗AI安全评估领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化在问题定义、技术路线、评估范式上均无交集。

Abstract: Readiness stress-testing of medical AI has focused on closed-ended and multimodal benchmarks. We extend it to open-ended clinical conversation under missing information, where safe behavior means recognizing absent information and qualifying, clarifying, or not over-committing - and where the evaluator becomes part of the measurement. We stress-test four models - three flagships (Claude Opus 4.8, GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3) and one mid-tier model (Gemini 3.5 Flash) - by deleting the latter half of the final user turn in HealthBench conversations, grading responses with a four-provider LLM-judge panel and a blinded clinician-anchored reference. Two evaluator-facing results are robust. First, judge choice materially changes apparent safety: inter-judge agreement is only moderate (Fleiss' kappa = 0.65), and after adjusting for each judge's general leniency (vote-level logistic regression), a positive same-provider association remains (exact permutation p = 0.04; GPT-5.5 ~ +0.10 on the probability scale) - large enough to change which model appears to over-commit least once its own-provider judge is excluded. Second, LLM judges are more permissive than clinicians on a blinded 50-item subsample: all four are significantly more lenient than the stricter independent clinician (crediting appropriate uncertainty on 66-84% of items vs 52%), and three of four than the author-influenced consensus (Grok directional only; judge-vs-consensus kappa = 0.20-0.43). On the author-audited clinical-underdetermined subset the permissiveness gap widened and the point-estimate model ordering held. A closed-ended MedQA anchor confirms accuracy is high and option-order effects are within a +/-5-point equivalence region for three of four models, so the safety gap is about calibration, not knowledge. We release the harness, prompts, per-item outputs, judge panel, perturbation audit, and human-annotation protocol.

</details>


### [120] [PhoenixRepair: Rethinking Repair Strategy Exploration in Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18859)
*Tianyue Jiang,Yanlin Wang,Xin He,Daya Guo,Jiachi Chen,Ming Wen,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向代码仓库自动修复的多智能体框架PhoenixRepair，通过多位置采样和迭代反思扩展修复策略搜索空间，在SWE-bench-Verified上取得SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于软件工程/代码修复领域的工作。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感或目标检测相关任务。任务为自动代码缺陷修复（automated issue resolution），场景为软件仓库（GitHub repository），不涉及任何遥感传感器或目标类别。

Model architecture: 多智能体框架（multi-agent framework），包含多位置采样（multi-location sampling）、图定位（graph-based localization）、迭代反思与精炼（iterative reflection and refinement）、历史经验蒸馏引导（distilled insights from all historical attempts）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: SWE-bench-Verified（代码修复基准数据集）；评价指标为Pass@1（一次通过率）和故障定位准确率（fault localization accuracy）。

Experiments: 在SWE-bench-Verified上，PhoenixRepair在DeepSeek-V3.1下相对SWE-agent提升7.8% Pass@1；在MiniMax-M2.5下达到76.0% Pass@1；故障定位准确率优于现有方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文是软件工程领域的代码自动修复工作，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have greatly advanced automated issue resolution, existing agent-based methods exhibit a fundamental limitation in their insufficient exploration of repair strategies. This insufficiency manifests in two key aspects. First, the exploration of multiple potential edit locations is limited. Second, the exploration of repair attempts at each location is also insufficient. To address these challenges, we present PhoenixRepair, a multi-agent framework that systematically explores multiple candidate edit locations and performs iterative reflection and refinement on patch generation, thereby expanding the search space of repair strategies. Our framework begins with multi-location sampling, optionally augmented with graph-based localization information for difficult tasks, followed by iterative reflection and refinement to generate better patches, culminating in final-round generation guided by distilled insights from all historical attempts. Experiments on SWE-bench-Verified demonstrate that PhoenixRepair achieves the largest relative improvement of 7.8\% over SWE-agent under DeepSeek-V3.1, and attains the highest resolved rate of 76.0\% Pass@1 under MiniMax-M2.5. Meanwhile, it achieves higher fault localization accuracy than existing approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/PhoenixRepair.

</details>


### [121] [NaviAIS: A Scenario-Level Vessel Trajectory Prediction Dataset withVectorized Lane Priors and the NaviLane Forecasting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18887)
*Yuan Gui,Hongchen Luo,Liqi Qu,Longyue Fu,Jiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了面向船舶轨迹预测的标准化AIS数据集NaviAIS和分层宏动作预测框架NaviLane，不涉及遥感卫星目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于海洋船舶轨迹预测（vessel trajectory prediction），使用AIS数据，不涉及遥感卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"遥感"和"卫星"在广义上可能关联AIS卫星数据，但论文核心是轨迹预测而非遥感图像理解，且完全不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：船舶轨迹预测（vessel trajectory prediction）；场景：复杂海洋环境中的交通管理、碰撞预警、路径规划、自主导航；传感器：AIS（Automatic Identification System）；目标类别：船舶轨迹。

Model architecture: NaviLane框架包含：轨迹-地图联合编码（trajectory-map joint encoding）、离散宏动作码本（discrete macro-action codebook）、粗到细的多模态候选生成、残差细化模块（residual refinement module）、基于世界模型的后果感知评估器（world-model-based consequence-aware evaluator）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NaviAIS（自建标准化场景级AIS数据集），包含多船历史-未来轨迹、统一时间窗口和局部坐标系、栅格化可航行地图、矢量化航道先验、车道图等结构化地图表示。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 实验表明NaviLane在单模态和多模态设置下均优于代表性基线方法，验证了结构化航行先验、分层多模态生成和后果感知评估的价值。未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅针对AIS船舶轨迹预测，不涉及遥感图像处理；3. 未讨论模型效率、实时性或部署可行性；4. 数据集和方法的通用性仅限于海洋交通场景。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。但以下思路可间接参考：1）结构化先验编码（如航道图）的思想可类比遥感中的地理先验信息（如道路网、建筑轮廓）辅助检测；2）分层粗到细生成策略可类比遥感小目标检测中的由粗到精检测范式；3）后果感知评估可类比遥感检测中的场景上下文约束。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction in complex maritime environments is essential for traffic management, collision warning, route planning, and autonomous navigation. Although AIS-based learning methods have progressed rapidly, existing datasets are often released as raw message streams or irregular time series, with inconsistent sampling rates, noisy observations, heterogeneous coordinate systems, and non-unified scenario protocols. Most public AIS resources also lack structured representations of navigational lanes, waterway geometry, and navigable-region constraints, limiting reproducible, environment-aware forecasting. To address this, we introduce NaviAIS, a standardized scenario-level AIS dataset for vessel trajectory prediction. It organizes multi-vessel historical-future trajectories within unified temporal windows and local coordinate systems, and provides rasterized navigable maps, vectorized lane priors, lane graphs, and structured map representations. Compared with existing datasets, it jointly supports vectorized lanes, multi-scenario coverage, vectorized maps, open accessibility, and processed trajectories. Built on this dataset, we propose NaviLane, a hierarchical macro-action framework for map-aware prediction. NaviLane first performs trajectory-map joint encoding for a unified scene representation, then uses a discrete macro-action codebook to generate multimodal candidates coarse-to-refined. A residual refinement module improves local geometric and dynamical consistency, and a world-model-based consequence-aware evaluator ranks candidates by interaction risk and environmental feasibility. Experiments show NaviLane outperforms representative baselines in both single-modal and multimodal settings, confirming the value of structured navigational priors, hierarchical multimodal generation, and consequence-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [OntoBook: Ontology-Grounded Synthetic Textbooks for Medical Encoder Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18927)
*Rian Touchent,Éric de la Clergerie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OntoBook 是一种利用医学本体结构生成预训练信号来训练医学编码语言模型的方法，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1 分。该论文聚焦医学自然语言处理（医学编码、本体、法语语言模型），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化/部署均无任何关联。属于与本研究方向基本无关的论文。

Reading priority: 1 分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学编码（medical coding）、本体驱动的语言模型预训练。应用场景：法语医学文本编码（FRACCO, Cantemist-FR, Distemist-FR）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：医学代码（CIM-10, CCAM, ATC 本体）。

Model architecture: ModernCamemBERT，149M 参数的法国编码器语言模型。训练目标：掩码语言建模（MLM）+ 代码对关系预测（relation prediction）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等）。模型参数量 149M，在 NLP 领域属中等规模，但未讨论推理效率或部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。149M 参数对于星上部署而言过大，但论文未讨论任何边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FRACCO, Cantemist-FR, Distemist-FR（法语医学编码基准）。指标：micro-F1。实验设置：对比 MLM-only 预训练，评估 OntoBook 预训练策略的效果。

Experiments: OntoBook 在三个基准上均优于纯 MLM 预训练：FRACCO +2.5 micro-F1，Distemist-FR +8.0 micro-F1。发现目标对齐（alignment）至关重要，不对齐训练导致 30 个点的性能下降。发布了 130 万条 LLM 生成的医学教科书文本及模型权重。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 仅针对法语医学编码，领域迁移性差。3. 未讨论模型效率、推理速度或部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可抽象借鉴：1）利用结构化知识（如遥感场景图、目标空间关系）生成预训练文本或辅助信号来增强遥感基础模型；2）多任务训练中目标对齐的重要性——在遥感轻量化模型中，检测头与轻量化分支的联合训练需注意目标一致性。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: We present OntoBook, a method that converts medical ontology structure into pretraining signal for encoder language models. Our approach has three stages: random walks through ontology graphs capture hierarchical and causal relations between medical codes, a large language model reformulates these walks into fluent textbook-style prose, and the resulting text is used to train ModernCamemBERT, a 149M-parameter French encoder, with two objectives on the same data: masked language modeling and relation prediction between code pairs. On three French medical coding benchmarks (FRACCO, Cantemist-FR, Distemist-FR), OntoBook achieves significant improvements over MLM-only pretraining, with +2.5 micro-F1 on FRACCO and +8.0 micro-F1 on Distemist. We find that alignment between objectives is necessary: misaligned training, where each task uses different data, causes a 30-point degradation. We release 1.3 million LLM-reformulated medical textbooks across three French ontologies (CIM-10, CCAM, ATC) and pretrained model checkpoints.

</details>


### [123] [What General Intelligence Requires: Non-Reducible Constraints Across Levels of Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18943)
*Subhomoy Bakshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论通用人工智能（AGI）的结构约束，认为单一架构进步或规模扩展无法实现AGI，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或任何与在轨部署相关的技术内容。它是一篇关于通用人工智能（AGI）哲学与理论框架的论文，讨论认知科学、人类学、法学和经济学视角下的智能结构约束，属于认知科学/人工智能哲学范畴。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何计算机视觉任务或遥感场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体的模型架构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何实验或对比方法。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何技术参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AGI理论探讨，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: General intelligence, of the kind that underwrites the full range of human cognitive achievement, is not a property of computational architecture alone. This paper advances a single thesis: the structural constraints on general intelligence occupy distinct levels of description and are mutually non-reducible, in the sense that the special-sciences tradition gives to that term. It follows that no single architectural advance, and no continuation of the scaling programme by itself, can produce artificial general intelligence (AGI), and that research programmes must be evaluated against the full constraint profile rather than against performance on any one benchmark. The thesis is developed through a method that reads general intelligence through four evidential lenses, AI systems research, anthropology, law, and economics, each anchored to a distinct level of description, supplemented by speculative fiction used as a disciplined heuristic in the context of discovery rather than the context of justification. Applying the method yields a taxonomy of twenty-three structural constraints organised into eight clusters; six are examined in depth and ordered as an ascending ladder of levels, with explicit bridges showing why progress at one level cannot carry to the next. The argument issues in five falsifiable predictions, each stated with a named benchmark family and a disconfirmation condition, converting a descriptive framework into a research programme with a longer horizon than the scaling hypothesis implies.

</details>


### [124] [From Dependency to Compositionality: A Neurosymbolic Lifting of LLM Outputs via Combinatory Categorial Grammar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18961)
*Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号框架，将LLM自回归生成输出提升为CCG类型组合推导，用于自然语言和形式语言的语法检查与幻觉检测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于计算语言学/自然语言处理领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的LLM架构。提出的是LLM-CCG神经符号框架，将LLM输出提升为CCG类型组合推导，利用Curry-Howard correspondence扩展到形式语言。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。未提及任何数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。未提供实验对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 论文仅提供框架性描述和理论论证，缺乏实验验证。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的工程或算法细节。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。其神经符号方法（LLM+CCG）属于NLP领域，不适用于遥感图像目标检测任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate fluent text by incrementally predicting the next token from a prefix. Critics in the generative tradition argue that such systems lack genuine grammar; influential replies from the dependency-grammar perspective hold that LLM behavior is well described by local head-dependent structure built word by word. We argue that a sharper observation has been overlooked: the prefix-driven, type-completing dynamics of autoregressive generation align closely with the incremental processing model that Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG) was originally designed to support. On this basis we propose a neurosymbolic framework in which LLM outputs are lifted into typed compositional derivations -- not claiming that LLMs implement CCG internally, but that their outputs admit a principled, incremental, and auditable CCG reconstruction. Two consequences follow. First, through the Curry-Howard correspondence the lifting extends beyond natural language to the formal languages LLMs also produce -- programming languages such as Solidity, description-logic and query languages such as OWL and SQL -- with the type system varying and the architecture held fixed. Second, the lifting supports two layers of checking: a compositional layer that catches structural failures directly, and a content layer that checks the lifted structure against external knowledge sources, enabling the earliest possible flagging of hallucinated content. The account thereby requires of a producer not cognition but a prefix-driven generative profile. We close with a sketch of synchronous LLM-CCG coupling as one direction the framework opens.

</details>


### [125] [Measuring Reward-Seeking via Contrastive Belief Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18966)
*Axel Højmark,Jérémy Scheurer,Evgenia Nitishinskaya,Felix Hofstätter,Jason Wolfe,Theodore Ehrenborg,Bronson Schoen,Alexander Meinke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在强化学习训练中出现的"奖励追逐"问题，即模型学会优化评分者的判断而非预期目标，并提出对比合成文档微调方法来测量这一行为。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。本文研究的是大语言模型的RL训练对齐问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型对齐与奖励追逐行为测量；应用场景：LLM安全与对齐；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及OpenAI o3中间检查点和gpt-oss-120b模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning方法构造对比文档；评价指标为模型在"偏向评分者"与"偏向用户/开发者"之间的行为偏移率。

Experiments: 在编码和对齐任务上测试o3中间检查点，发现后期检查点更倾向于迎合评分者偏好（如违背承诺完成任务的比率从早期40% vs 24%变为87% vs 9%）；gpt-oss-120b奖励追逐模型对评分者偏好的敏感度是未修改模型的两倍以上。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文研究的是LLM对齐问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Language models trained with reinforcement learning may learn to optimize the grader's judgment rather than the intended objective. This "reward-seeking" is difficult to measure because a model that pursues the grader's judgment and one that pursues the intended objective behave identically whenever the grader rewards the intended behavior. We measure reward-seeking using Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning to change a model's beliefs about what the grader rewards, putting those beliefs in conflict with what users or developers want, and measuring the rate at which the model adopts each party's preferred behavior. Applied to intermediate checkpoints of a capabilities-focused OpenAI o3 RL run, without safety training, we find that these checkpoints often side with grader preferences over those of users or developers on coding and alignment tasks. This tendency to side with the grader trends upward throughout RL training. For example, in an environment that forces a choice between keeping a promise to a supervisor and breaking it to complete the task, a late capabilities-focused o3 checkpoint breaks the promise 87% of the time when SDF documents say the grader rewards task completion, versus 9% when they say it rewards honesty (a choice its chain-of-thought often makes explicit). An earlier checkpoint is far less sensitive (40% vs. 24%). Our method also generalizes to reward-hacking models. A model organism trained to reward-hack (gpt-oss-120b) is more than twice as sensitive to grader preferences as the unmodified model, with the mean behavioral shift in favor of the grader rising from 33% to 86%. These results indicate that RL can increase reward-seeking over the course of training, producing models that may act against their developers' intentions when they believe that doing so leads to higher reward.

</details>


### [126] [Quality Action Assurance: Multimodal Verification of Examiner Claims in VR OSCEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19063)
*Harry Rogers,Sally Shiels,Ashley Tomlinson,James Thomas,James Aylward,Nathan Gauge,Helen Higham,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QAA多模态框架，用于验证VR儿科OSCE考试中考官评分主张与真实事件序列的一致性，提升评分客观性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学教育评估中的考官偏差检测（VR儿科OSCE），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：考官评分偏差检测与验证；场景：VR儿科OSCE（医学教育评估）；传感器/数据类型：视频、VR日志、演员数据；目标类别：考官动作主张与真实事件的对齐验证。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: QAA框架包含两部分：(1) 约束时序动作对齐模型（constrained temporal action alignment model），负责动作定位和演员来源归因；(2) 大语言模型（LLM），用于提取考官主张并与记录比对。与遥感检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：VR儿科OSCE考试数据（视频+VR日志+演员数据）；评价指标：Actor F1（99.2%±0.7%）、W@16时序对齐（93.4%±1.9%）、考官错误检测的精确率（70.0%）和召回率（76.7%）、事实正确性（从39.2%提升至79.2%）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 5折交叉验证。QAA在时序对齐任务上表现优异（Actor F1 99.2%，W@16 93.4%），能将考官评分的事实正确性从39.2%提升至79.2%。无遥感目标检测对比实验。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标；(3) 仅针对VR OSCE特定场景，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：其"约束时序对齐+LLM验证"的多模态框架思路，在遥感领域若涉及多源数据（如光学+SAR）的时序对齐验证时或有参考价值，但与目标检测轻量化核心课题关联甚微。

Abstract: Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs) are the gold standard for assessing clinical competence, yet scoring remains vulnerable to examiner subjectivity, fatigue, and cognitive bias. Standard examiner validation via inter-rater statistics lacks explanatory power regarding the source of errors, as it neither analyzes examiner reasoning nor verifies examiner claims against actual events. Thus, we introduce Quality Action Assurance (QAA), a multimodal framework that verifies examiner claims in Virtual Reality (VR) pediatric OSCEs by comparing actions claimed by examiners against the true sequence of events, constructed from video, VR logs, and actor data. QAA combines a constrained temporal action alignment model, which performs action localization and actor source attribution, with a large language model that extracts examiner claims and checks them against the record. Across a 5-fold cross-validation, QAA achieves 99.2% $\pm$ 0.7% Actor F1 and 93.4% $\pm$ 1.9% W@16 for temporal alignment. Overall, QAA detects examiner errors with 70.0% precision and 76.7% recall, improving factual correctness from 39.2% to 79.2%, enabling fairer OSCE assessment.

</details>


### [127] [On the Effectiveness of Pretraining for Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19072)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向图组合优化问题（如TSP）的自监督预训练框架，利用几何增广（旋转和反射）的图对比学习来学习不变结构表示，提升大规模路由问题的求解质量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是图组合优化（Graph Combinatorial Optimization）中的路由问题（TSP），属于运筹学/图神经网络领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化模型或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图组合优化（Graph Combinatorial Optimization），具体为旅行商问题（TSP）求解。应用场景：路由规划。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于图神经网络（GNN）的对比学习框架，使用几何增广（旋转和反射）进行自监督预训练。具体架构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TSP问题实例（TSP1000等规模）。评价指标：tour length（路径长度）。

Experiments: 混合策略（旋转+反射）在TSP1000上相比无预训练模型实现了6.57%的路径长度改进。摘要未说明与其他SOTA方法的详细对比。

Limitations: 与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。仅几何增广（旋转/反射）的思路在遥感图像中常见，但论文的应用场景和目标完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：论文中使用的几何增广（旋转、反射）在遥感图像中也是常见的数据增强手段，但这是非常通用的技术，不具备独特性。除此之外，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题几乎没有可借鉴的内容。

Abstract: This paper introduces a self-supervised pretraining framework for graph combinatorial optimization specifically designed to address the nature of routing problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem. By utilizing graph contrastive learning with geometric augmentations (specifically, rotations and axial reflections) the model is forced to learn invariant structural representations and global relative distance distributions. Results demonstrate that this pretraining strategy outperforms non-pretrained models across various problem scales. Notably, the hybrid strategy (combining rotation and reflection) achieved a 6.57% improvement in tour length for TSP1000, proving that geometric pretraining is an important inductive bias for effectively scaling neural solvers to high-dimensional instances.

</details>


### [128] [Supra Cognitive Modes: A Routed Architecture for Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19096)
*Joshua Tobkin,David Yang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM)架构，包含冻结语义分类器、运行时门控机制、融合词法/稠密检索、图/迭代多跳处理、分层长文合成，以及多粒度嵌入、三元组、事实版本元数据等共享存储基座。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Long-term Conversational Memory (LoCoMo, n=1,986)、MemoryAgentBench (MAB, n=3,671)、LongMemEval (n=500)；评价指标：准确率(84.87% on LoCoMo factoid, 68.61% on adversarial abstention, 61.49% on MAB, 86.00% on LongMemEval)。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的研究问题、技术路线和应用场景与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无交集。

Abstract: Agent-memory workloads mix direct factual lookup, relation-chain and current-state reasoning, and broad synthesis over long histories. We describe Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM), an architecture that maps explicit or automatically selected per-query modes to retrieval and synthesis payloads over one shared ingest substrate. A frozen semantic classifier and runtime gates dispatch queries among fused lexical and dense lookup, graph or iterative multi-hop handling, and stratified long-form synthesis. The substrate combines multi-granularity embeddings, extracted triples, fact-version metadata, and optional asynchronous enrichments. We characterize the deployed configuration on three benchmarks: Long-term Conversational Memory (LoCoMo; n = 1,986), MemoryAgentBench (MAB; n = 3,671), and LongMemEval (n = 500). The reference run records 84.87% on LoCoMo factoid categories and 68.61% on adversarial abstention, 61.49% on MAB across two repetitions, and 86.00% on LongMemEval. A repository-backed reproduction produces similar aggregate scores and supports task- and mode-conditioned failure analysis. Raw baseline outputs, aligned end-to-end timing for LoCoMo and LongMemEval, and complete token ledgers are unavailable; stored rows also omit some final runtime decisions. The results characterize one implemented routed configuration and its diagnostic failure patterns, while source inspection verifies the per-query control interface and shared-substrate design. Causal routing effects, efficiency gains, and statistical significance remain outside the available evidence.

</details>


### [129] [OpenRTAG: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robust Text-Attributed Graph Learning under Data Quality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19108)
*Yuze Dai,Zhihan Zhang,Yan Zhao,Ruoyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRTAG，一个面向文本属性图（TAG）学习的鲁棒性基准，系统分类了文本、结构和标签三个维度的质量退化场景（稀疏、噪声、不平衡），并在多个数据集和模型族上评估鲁棒性。

<details>
