### [139] [FSDBN: Foreground-Aware EEG--Visual Alignment via Dynamic Brain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18344)
*Yiheng Liu,Chuhang Zheng,Peiliang Gong,Jingtao Liu,Daoqiang Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FSDBN框架，利用EEG信号进行视觉语义解码，通过显著性对齐和动态门控融合实现前景-背景分离，用于零样本脑-图像检索。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是EEG脑电信号视觉解码（brain-to-image retrieval），属于脑机接口与视觉语义理解交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究EEG脑电信号解码，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：EEG-based visual decoding（脑电视觉解码）、zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval（零样本脑-图像检索）；场景：非遥感，属于脑机接口；传感器：EEG（脑电图）；目标类别：视觉语义中的前景/背景，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: FSDBN框架，包含Semantic-Consistent Saliency Alignment（语义一致显著性对齐）和Semantic-Prior Dynamic Gating Foreground Fusion（语义先验动态门控前景融合），以及adaptive spatiotemporal brain networks（自适应时空脑网络）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：top-1 accuracy (69.0%)、top-5 accuracy (92.2%)，用于zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval任务。

Experiments: 在zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval任务上，FSDBN达到69.0% top-1准确率和92.2% top-5准确率，优于此前SOTA方法。摘要未提供消融实验或对比方法名称。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感、目标检测或轻量化技术信息。EEG信号处理与遥感图像处理在数据模态、任务目标、部署约束上差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其前景-背景分离的注意力机制思路（如显著性对齐、动态门控融合）在通用视觉领域有一定参考价值，但与本课题的遥感目标检测+轻量化核心方向不匹配。

Abstract: EEG-based visual decoding provides a non-invasive pathway for interpreting visual semantics. However, existing methods often overlook the perceptual asymmetry between foreground and background in complex scenes, leading to background interference and semantic misalignment. EEG signals also exhibit rapid temporal dynamics and nonstationary spatial patterns, making it difficult to capture the time-varying brain connectivity associated with focal visual attention. To address these limitations, we propose FSDBN, a unified framework for robust EEG-visual decoding. FSDBN introduces Semantic-Consistent Saliency Alignment to separate semantically relevant foreground regions from background noise under joint saliency and semantic constraints. It further employs Semantic-Prior Dynamic Gating Foreground Fusion to adaptively regulate the contributions of foreground and background features. In parallel, EEG signals are modeled as adaptive spatiotemporal brain networks whose functional connectivity dynamically reorganizes to capture neural responses to salient foregrounds. Experiments on zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval demonstrate that FSDBN achieves 69.0 percent top-1 accuracy and 92.2 percent top-5 accuracy, outperforming previous state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/LiuYiheng1/FSDBN-EEG.

</details>


### [140] [Thinking Fast, Thinking Slow: Adaptive Multimodal Transformer-based Sensor Fusion for Depth Estimation on Ultra-low-power MCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18897)
*Luca Crupi,Lorenzo Lamberti,Giovanni Badaracco,Daniele Allegri,Alessandro Giusti,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向超低功耗嵌入式平台的自适应多模态传感器融合方法，结合轻量级循环Transformer架构（688k参数）和置信度门控机制，在NYUv2深度估计任务上以极低能耗（2.44 mJ/frame）实现接近MobileDepth的精度。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于室内深度估计（depth map estimation）和超低功耗嵌入式多模态融合（camera+ultrasound+ToF），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然使用了轻量Transformer（688k参数）和GAP9 SoC部署，但轻量化方法是为室内深度估计服务的，与遥感在轨目标检测无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。仅置信度门控自适应推理和超低功耗SoC部署方案有极有限的间接参考价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：深度图估计（depth map estimation）；场景：室内超低功耗嵌入式/物联网场景；传感器：camera, ultrasound, Time-of-Flight；目标类别：无目标检测，仅深度估计。

Model architecture: 轻量级循环Transformer架构（688k参数），结合token propagation（跨时间步传播上下文特征）和置信度门控机制（confidence-based gating），实现增量式传感器融合。

Lightweight method: 模型轻量化方面：参数量仅688k，属于轻量网络设计；部署方面：使用ULP GWT GAP9多核SoC（超低功耗芯片），实现2.44 mJ/frame的超低能耗。但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 参数量：688k；能耗：2.44 mJ/frame（自适应模式），平均功耗约400 mW；部署平台：GAP9 SoC（超低功耗多核芯片）。但这是室内嵌入式场景，非星上部署。未说明FLOPs、延迟、显存等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NYUv2（室内深度估计数据集）；评价指标：δ1 accuracy（深度估计精度指标）。

Experiments: 自适应系统相比全传感器全迭代方案，δ1 accuracy仅下降4.8%，但节省90%能耗（2.44 mJ/frame）；相比MobileDepth，参数量少9倍，δ1 accuracy仅低5.6%；相比同样运行在GAP9上的SOTA模型，δ1 accuracy提升31.8%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，场景、任务、传感器类型均不匹配；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及遥感图像特有的挑战（如大尺度变化、旋转目标、复杂背景等）；4. 未涉及星上部署的约束（如辐射环境、存储限制、实时性要求等）。

Ideas for my research: 1. 置信度门控机制（confidence-based gating）的思路可借鉴：在遥感在轨场景中，可根据图像质量或目标置信度动态决定是否启用更复杂的检测分支或更多计算资源，实现自适应推理；2. token propagation跨时间步传播特征的方法，可用于遥感视频流或时序遥感数据的轻量处理；3. 688k参数的轻量Transformer设计思路对星上模型设计有参考价值；4. GAP9 SoC的超低功耗部署方案（~400 mW）可作为星上边缘计算硬件的参考基线。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-based multimodal sensor fusion is a relevant topic gaining ever more traction across ultra-low-power (ULP) embedded and cyber-physical systems, as it improves reliability, accuracy, and robustness under real-world constraints. However, adding more and more sensors to ultra-constrained sub-100 mW platforms requires balancing energy consumption against prediction accuracy. To achieve this ambitious goal, we present a novel adaptive AI methodology that combines multimodal sensor fusion (camera, ultrasound, and Time-of-Flight sensors) with a lightweight recurrent Transformer-based architecture (688 k parameters). We address the depth map estimation task with a mechanism that combines token propagation across iterations with incremental sensor utilization. At each iteration, a confidence-based gating mechanism dynamically decides whether to continue the computation by adding progressively richer but more power-demanding sensors as input. Token propagation ensures temporal consistency by forwarding context features across time. To deploy our algorithm and test a first real-world prototype, we design a novel printed circuit board featuring all three sensors, coupled with an ULP GWT GAP9 multicore System-on-Chip. When comparing our adaptive system against the same pipeline using all sensors and iterations on the NYUv2 dataset, we lose only 4.8% of the δ1 accuracy in exchange for 90% energy saving (2.44 mJ/frame). Finally, our adaptive method marks only 5.6% lower δ1 accuracy than MobileDepth despite using 9x fewer parameters. Compared with a state-of-the-art model also running on GAP9, our method improves δ1 accuracy by 31.8% thanks to our adaptive sensor fusion while operating within the same average power budget (~400 mW).

</details>


### [141] [Wavefront Parallelization for Efficient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19082)
*Shimon Murai,Fangzheng Lin,Kasidis Arunruangsirilert,Jiro Katto*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重新训练、基于波前并行（wavefront parallelism）的推理加速算法，用于加速预训练的自回归图像压缩模型，在保持率失真性能的同时实现13倍以上加速。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文核心是图像压缩（learned image compression）的推理加速，属于底层视觉/编码领域。虽然图像压缩与遥感图像传输、星上数据压缩有一定关联，但论文完全不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等），仅关注自回归模型的串行推理加速。对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义有限。

Reading priority: 1分——与课题方向弱相关。建议仅作为图像压缩加速的泛读材料，不必深入精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：学习型图像压缩（learned image compression）的推理加速；应用场景：通用图像压缩，非遥感/卫星场景；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于自回归上下文模型（autoregressive context models）的压缩模型（如Cheng et al.的模型）。提出wavefront并行推理顺序，将串行解码重组为交错（staggered）波前顺序。

Lightweight method: 非传统轻量化方法。提出的是推理阶段加速算法（inference-time acceleration），无需训练、无需改变网络架构。核心思想借鉴视频编码标准中的波前并行（wavefront parallelism），通过优化解码顺序减少串行步骤。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了加速比（13×以上），但这是在通用GPU上的推理加速，非星上/边缘部署场景。波前并行方法理论上可利用多核处理器并行加速，但未讨论FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等嵌入式部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：率失真性能（rate-distortion performance）和加速比（speedup ratio）。

Experiments: 在预训练的自回归模型（Cheng et al.）上实现13×以上加速，且保持原始率失真性能。同时指出可以通过牺牲部分上下文依赖关系来获得更高解码速度。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化（参数量/FLOPs压缩）；4. 仅针对自回归图像压缩模型的解码加速，适用范围窄；5. 未讨论在资源受限设备（如星上处理器）上的实际部署可行性；6. 加速效果依赖于并行硬件资源，单核/低算力设备上可能收益有限。

Ideas for my research: 1. 波前并行的思想可借鉴到遥感图像在轨处理中的其他串行推理环节（如序列化解码、自回归检测头等）的加速；2. 训练无关（training-free）的加速策略对星上部署有吸引力，因为星上模型通常无法重新训练；3. 但整体而言，该论文与"遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接相关性较低，建议仅作为并行加速方法的背景参考。

Abstract: Autoregressive context models are foundational for learned image compression,but they suffer from slow serial inference. Existing acceleration methods such as checkerboard context require architectural changes and retraining, thus are inapplicable to pre-trained models. We propose a completely training-free inference-time acceleration algorithm inspired by wavefront parallelism in video coding standards. Our method reorganizes inference into an optimal ``staggered'' wavefront order, minimizing sequential steps while maintaining exact autoregressive dependencies. Experimental results show our approach accelerates pre-trained autoregressive models (e.g., Cheng et al.) by more than $13\times$ while preserving the original rate-distortion performance. We also demonstrate that faster decoding is possible by trading off precise context dependencies. Source code will be available at https://github.com/tokkiwa/compressai-wavefront.

</details>


### [142] [Fast reconstruction of tensor tomographic X-ray scattering data for real-time applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19124)
*André M. Antunes,Daniël M. Pelt,K. Joost Batenburg*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于代数滤波器的直接重建方法，将X射线散射张量断层扫描的迭代重建加速一个数量级以上，实现53×53×53×28张量体在1秒内重建。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于X射线散射张量断层扫描（X-ray scattering tensor tomography）领域，涉及的是材料科学或生物医学成像中的3D纳米结构取向重建，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱遥感、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：X射线散射张量断层扫描的快速直接重建；应用场景：材料科学/生物医学的高通量成像分析；传感器类型：X射线散射（small-angle X-ray scattering）；目标类别：纳米尺度结构取向（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 提出一种扩展的直接重建方法，针对平行束几何（parallel-beam geometries），通过计算代数滤波器（algebraic filters）将多次迭代更新近似为单次滤波和反投影步骤。将断层投影算子（tomographic projector）与视角依赖的混合算子（view-dependent mixing operator）显式分离。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统模型轻量化方法。加速手段是通过算法层面的代数滤波器近似替代迭代重建，属于计算加速而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到在商用硬件上1秒完成53×53×53×28张量体重建，但未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了模拟数据（Simulated）和实验数据（experimental results）；评价指标：重建近似程度（approximates iterative reconstructions）、计算时间（computation time reduced by over an order of magnitude）。

Experiments: 模拟和实验结果表明该方法能近似迭代重建结果，同时计算时间降低一个数量级以上。具体重建了一个53×53×53×28的张量体，在商用硬件上耗时1秒。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对平行束几何（parallel-beam geometries），泛化性有限；2. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；3. 未涉及模型轻量化核心方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；4. 未讨论在资源受限平台上的部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发是：该工作通过代数滤波器将多次迭代近似为单步操作，这种"将迭代过程显式展开为可学习/可计算的滤波器"的思路，在遥感目标检测的轻量化中或许可以类比——例如将检测头中的多次卷积/上采样操作合并为等效的单步操作以减少计算量。但整体而言，方向差异过大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: X-ray scattering tensor tomography reveals nanoscale structural orientation in 3D, but its reliance on slow iterative reconstruction limits real-time use. We introduce an extension of a direct reconstruction approach for parallel-beam geometries that computes algebraic filters approximating multiple iterative updates with a single filtering and back-projection step. By explicitly separating the tomographic projector from a view-dependent mixing operator, the method generalizes across tensor representations and modes of (small-angle) X-ray scattering measurement. Simulated and experimental results show that the approach approximates iterative reconstructions while reducing computation time by over an order of magnitude, realizing a reconstruction of a 53x53x53x28 tensor volume in 1 second on commercially-available hardware. The resulting speed and stability enable high-throughput analysis and open the door to real-time scattering-based imaging.

</details>


### [143] [MIRAGE: Multi-scale Lesion-Informed Representation with Auxiliary Guidance for MRI Contrast Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19137)
*Andrea Borghesi,Xin Wang,Jonas Teuwen,George Yiasemis*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MIRAGE残差2D U-Net，通过病灶感知损失（非对称惩罚、多尺度分割、冻结nnU-Net指导）增强乳腺MRI对比度，在MAMA-SYNTH数据集上优于pix2pix和扩散模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学影像（乳腺MRI对比度增强），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测（病灶分割≠目标检测）；不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像对比度增强（乳腺MRI）；场景：医学影像分析；传感器：MRI（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：肿瘤病灶（非遥感目标如舰船/飞机/车辆）。

Model architecture: 残差2D U-Net（MIRAGE），结合全局重建损失、感知损失、三种病灶感知监督（非对称肿瘤增强惩罚、多尺度辅助肿瘤分割、冻结的nnU-Net分割指导）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MAMA-SYNTH（多中心，301例）；评价指标：8种互补指标（图像级、区域级、影像组学、分割级），包括LPIPS、对比分类等。

Experiments: 在6/8指标上排名第一；与pix2pix、条件扩散、潜在桥匹配基线对比；消融实验（留一进/留一出）显示损失函数部分冗余但各有侧重。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关；医学图像增强方法对遥感场景无直接迁移性；未涉及任何轻量化或部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。病灶感知损失的设计思路（非对称惩罚、多尺度辅助任务）在遥感目标检测中可能有启发，但本文不涉及遥感场景，且不涉及轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Inferring contrast enhancement from one pre-contrast breast MRI slice is underdetermined: post-contrast appearance contains physiological information that is not uniquely encoded in baseline anatomy. Optimizing only paired pixel fidelity can suppress uncertain lesion enhancement, whereas adversarial or stochastic generative objectives can favor realistic post-contrast appearance without guaranteeing patient-specific lesion fidelity. We introduce MIRAGE, a residual 2D U-Net that combines global reconstruction and perceptual losses with three forms of lesion-aware supervision available only during training: an asymmetric penalty for missed tumor enhancement, multi-scale auxiliary tumor segmentation, and guidance through a frozen post-contrast tumor segmentation nnU-Net. We evaluate the method on 301 cases from the multi-centre MAMA-SYNTH data using eight complementary image-, region-, radiomics-, and segmentation-based metrics. MIRAGE ranks first on six metrics and markedly improves downstream lesion localization over tuned pix2pix, conditional diffusion, and latent bridge-matching baselines. The generative alternatives retain advantages in LPIPS or contrast classification, revealing a clear fidelity-utility trade-off. Leave-one-in and leave-one-out ablations show that the losses are partly redundant for lesion localization but exert distinct effects on appearance, radiomics, and boundary accuracy. These results support task-aware synthesis while also showing that its apparent optimality is conditional on the downstream models and metrics used to define utility.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FALCON-Discover框架，通过置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性等差异信号来发现模型预测中"假自信集中"的危险错误区域，将危险过度自信视为发现问题而非校准问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用机器学习模型（XGBoost、CatBoost）的置信度校准与危险错误发现，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"置信度校准"概念在目标检测中有一定关联，但论文本身完全面向表格数据分类任务，与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关，阅读优先级最低。仅当需要研究检测模型置信度校准与可靠性评估时可作为背景参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：置信度校准与危险错误发现（false-confidence concentration detection）；应用场景：通用表格数据分类；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: FALCON-Discover框架：后处理、模型无关的排序框架，使用四种差异信号——置信度（confidence）、局部支持（local support）、邻域一致性（neighborhood agreement）、扰动稳定性（perturbation stability）。基学习器为XGBoost和CatBoost。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：7个二值表格数据集（binary tabular datasets）；实验设置：4个随机种子、5折交叉拟合（five-fold cross-fitting）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，但提到"discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline"。

Experiments: 主要发现：假自信集中（false-confidence concentration）是普遍存在的但依赖于数据分布；在主要置信度阈值下，基于差异信号的排序显著优于验证集选择的校准或信任评分基线；最佳检测器因数据集而异——学习型差异信号在需要组合多个线索时最强，而稳定性中心排序在局部决策脆弱性主导时最佳。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对二值表格数据，未验证在图像、遥感等复杂高维数据上的有效性；2. 仅使用XGBoost和CatBoost，未涉及深度学习模型；3. 摘要未说明计算开销，差异信号的计算可能带来额外推理成本；4. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但以下概念可间接参考：1. "危险错误发现"的思路可迁移到遥感目标检测中——检测模型在特定场景（如复杂背景、小目标）下可能产生高置信度的错误预测，可设计类似差异信号来识别这些"不可信"检测结果；2. 置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性、扰动稳定性这四类信号可作为遥感检测模型可靠性评估的参考维度；3. 但整体而言，该论文的方法和实验设置与遥感在轨轻量化目标检测差距较大，不建议直接复现。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于频谱特征与输出置信度融合的时间序列分类可靠性估计方法，通过验证门控策略在保持原模型预测不变的前提下提升可信度评估。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦时间序列分类（time-series classification）的可靠性校准（post-hoc calibration），完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"部署决策（deployment decisions）"一词出现，但讨论的是时间序列分类器的信任/弃权策略，而非遥感在轨目标检测模型的轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列分类的可靠性估计（reliability estimation for time-series classifiers）；应用场景：通用时间序列分类；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。摘要未说明具体时间序列数据类型。

Model architecture: 方法框架：validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy，保持backbone预测不变，融合output-side cues与whole-sample spectral descriptors（包括band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, phase stability）。使用validation gate决定是否启用频谱条件化。摘要未说明backbone网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文讨论的是可靠性校准方法，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提及"deployment decisions"，但指的是分类器的信任/弃权决策，而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：8个UCR/UEA时间序列数据集；评价指标：Corr-AURC（Correctness-Area Under Risk-Coverage Curve）、FalseConf@0.9、AURC；backbone：8种时间序列模型家族。

Experiments: 无约束方法在匹配评估子集上将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。与标准recalibrators对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，无法直接迁移；2. 未讨论计算开销和实时性；3. 仅在UCR/UEA时间序列数据集上验证，缺乏遥感数据验证；4. 未涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）"validation-gated"策略思想——在遥感在轨检测中，可设计类似门控机制，根据资源约束或置信度动态切换轻量/重模型；2）频谱特征（spectral descriptors）用于遥感多光谱/高光谱数据可能有天然适配性，但本文针对的是时间序列而非光谱图像；3）可靠性估计（reliability estimation）思路可用于在轨检测结果的置信度评估，辅助星上决策（如是否下传数据）。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合低秩近似、通道剪枝与逐token动态跳层的复合稀疏化框架，用于大语言模型压缩，探索参数稀疏与计算稀疏的协同效应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的压缩，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然其剪枝(pruning)和动态计算方法对轻量化有参考价值，但应用场景和任务类型与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向差异极大。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关。虽然复合稀疏的压缩思路有方法论上的参考价值，但论文完全面向NLP领域的LLM，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、星上部署等核心要素，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型压缩（语言理解与建模）；应用场景：NLP；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 复合稀疏框架：先通过低秩近似(low-rank approximation)和通道剪枝(channel pruning)获得静态压缩主干，再引入轻量路由器(lightweight routers)实现逐token动态层跳过(per-token dynamic layer skipping)。

Lightweight method: 静态压缩：低秩近似 + 通道剪枝；动态压缩：逐token动态层跳过；复合策略：参数稀疏(parameter sparsity)与token级计算稀疏(token-level computation sparsity)独立控制。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向LLM而非遥感模型，未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署硬件。

Datasets and metrics: 语言理解与建模基准(language understanding and modeling benchmarks)，具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 复合稀疏在相同总稀疏度下始终优于单一机制压缩，延缓理解任务上的性能衰减点，保持更强的建模性能。近平衡分配在固定稀疏预算下最有效。揭示了参数剪枝与token跳层之间的跨维度干扰(cross-dimensional interference)。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM，未在视觉模型或遥感目标检测模型上验证；2. 摘要未说明实际推理加速效果；3. 跨维度稀疏边界(cross-dimensional sparsity boundary)最终限制了进一步压缩，未给出突破方案；4. 未讨论在边缘/星上硬件上的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 1. 复合稀疏思想可借鉴：将静态剪枝（如通道剪枝）与动态计算（如根据输入复杂度跳过某些层或通道）结合，可能比单一轻量化方法更有效；2. 在遥感目标检测模型中，不同图像（如海洋vs城市）复杂度差异大，动态计算策略有潜力；3. 低秩近似+通道剪枝的静态压缩可作为遥感检测模型的基础轻量化手段；4. 需注意跨维度干扰问题，在遥感场景下需重新调优分配比例。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [147] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对称α稳定谱成分的自适应高斯过程核族ALAS，用于贝叶斯优化中的黑箱目标函数建模。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于贝叶斯优化/高斯过程核方法的基础研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为贝叶斯优化中的黑箱目标函数建模，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。提出ALAS和ALAS-Sep两种高斯过程核参数化形式，基于对称α稳定谱成分，可自适应学习平滑度参数α。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未出现）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。实验在标准基准测试和真实代理函数上进行，评价指标未明确列出。

Experiments: 实验表明ALAS和ALAS-Sep在多种设置下表现强劲且鲁棒，但未与遥感目标检测方法进行对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测模型、轻量化方法或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于贝叶斯优化领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [148] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCC框架，将冻结的DINOv2骨干网络与轻量YOLO检测头结合，并引入LoRA模块实现参数高效联邦学习，用于胎儿超声图像中胼胝体定位，在降低通信开销的同时保持较高检测精度。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。该论文涉及目标检测（YOLO-based detection head）和轻量化方法（LoRA参数高效微调、轻量检测头），但应用场景是**胎儿超声医学图像**，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关。轻量化思路（LoRA、冻结骨干+轻量头）对模型压缩有借鉴意义，但非遥感场景，且不涉及在轨部署、实时推理、边缘部署等星上相关约束。

Reading priority: 2分——阅读优先级较低。虽然LoRA+轻量检测头的参数高效微调思路有一定借鉴意义，但论文场景（医学超声）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测差距较大，且缺乏推理效率、FLOPs、部署相关指标。建议仅快速浏览LoRA和冻结骨干部分，无需精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：目标检测（object detection），具体为胎儿超声图像中胼胝体（corpus callosum）定位。应用场景：产前神经超声检查（fetal neurosonography），多中心临床环境。传感器类型：超声成像（ultrasound imaging）。目标类别：胼胝体（单一类别）。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: 整体框架FedCC：冻结的DINOv2骨干网络（frozen backbone）+ 轻量YOLO检测头（lightweight YOLO-based detection head）。引入LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）模块实现参数高效微调。联邦学习策略采用FedAvg。

Lightweight method: 1. 冻结DINOv2骨干网络，仅训练轻量YOLO检测头；2. 引入LoRA模块，仅优化和交换少量参数（2.9M可训练参数 vs 全微调24.4M），通信成本降低约8.5倍。未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS等方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（仅给出可训练参数2.9M，总参数量未说明）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向的是资源受限的临床环境（resource-constrained clinical settings），而非星上/边缘部署场景。轻量化主要体现在通信开销降低，而非推理效率优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多中心数据集，包含10,970张超声帧，来自58名孕妇，3个临床站点，使用不同成像设备。评价指标：mAP@50（平均精度均值，IoU阈值0.5）、F1-score。未使用遥感领域常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）。

Experiments: FedAvg + DINOv2 + LoRA组合在联邦设置下达到平均mAP@50为0.857，F1-score为0.803。对比基线包括全微调（full fine-tuning）和冻结编码器（encoder-freezing）。可训练参数从24.4M降至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星方向完全无关，属于医学图像分析领域；2. 未涉及推理速度、FLOPs、模型大小等边缘/星上部署关键指标；3. 仅针对单一目标类别（胼胝体）检测，未验证多类别场景；4. 联邦学习设置与单机星上部署场景差异较大；5. 未涉及量化、剪枝等更激进的轻量化手段。

Ideas for my research: 1. **冻结大模型骨干+轻量检测头**的思路可迁移到遥感领域：例如冻结预训练的遥感基础模型（如Swin Transformer、ViT）作为特征提取器，搭配轻量检测头（如YOLO head），减少可训练参数；2. **LoRA参数高效微调**可应用于遥感目标检测模型的领域自适应（如从自然图像到遥感图像的迁移），在保持遥感大模型能力的同时降低微调成本；3. **联邦学习+轻量化**的组合思路对多星协同场景有启发，但需注意联邦学习与单星在轨推理的差异较大；4. 该论文的轻量化程度（8.5倍通信降低）在遥感星上场景中参考价值有限，需结合更激进的压缩方法（量化、剪枝）。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [149] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合参数重要性与逐层功能等价的SVD低秩近似方法，并设计了一种高效的动态压缩率分配算法，用于大语言模型的神经网络压缩。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的SVD压缩，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或任何视觉任务。虽然SVD低秩近似是一种通用的模型压缩技术，但论文的应用场景（LLM）、方法设计（针对Transformer权重矩阵）和实验设置均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，仅SVD压缩思想有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型（LLM）压缩；应用场景：资源受限环境下的LLM部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于SVD（奇异值分解）的低秩近似压缩方法，针对NN的权重矩阵进行分解。方法结合了参数重要性（parameter importance）和逐层功能等价（per-layer functional equivalence）两种视角，并提出了动态压缩率分配算法。

Lightweight method: SVD低秩近似（矩阵分解类压缩方法）；动态压缩率分配（dynamic compression rate allocation），替代均匀分配或启发式搜索。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。论文针对的是LLM在资源受限环境下的内存压缩，但未涉及星上或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验结果表明所提方法在性能上持平或显著优于先前SOTA方法，尤其在高压缩比下表现突出。但摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM场景，未在视觉模型（如CNN、ViT）上验证；2. 未涉及遥感图像或目标检测任务；3. 未讨论在轨部署的硬件约束（如FPGA/NPU/TensorRT适配）；4. 缺乏参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. SVD低秩近似方法本身可作为遥感目标检测模型轻量化的候选技术，但需适配CNN/Transformer检测头；2. 动态压缩率分配的思想可借鉴到遥感检测模型中不同层（如backbone vs. neck vs. head）的非均匀剪枝/量化策略；3. 结合参数重要性与功能等价的双视角优化目标，可迁移到遥感检测模型的剪枝或蒸馏损失设计中。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为论文未涉及遥感场景和检测任务。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [150] [PAC--Bayes Bounds on Quotient Parameter Spaces: Geometry-induced Implicit-Bias Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18422)
*Nicola Aladrah,Fabio Anselmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在商空间（quotient predictor space）上进行PAC-Bayesian分析，通过构造隐式偏置先验来消除参数对称性导致的KL散度冗余，从而得到更紧的泛化界。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是纯机器学习理论工作，讨论PAC-Bayesian泛化界与参数对称性，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无任何直接或间接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体视觉任务或遥感场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅在实验中提及Fourier-Hadamard参数化回归和Query-Key attention（注意力机制）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是PAC-Bayes理论分析，与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为商空间KL散度和PAC-Bayes证书（certificate）的均值。

Experiments: 在Fourier-Hadamard回归和Query-Key attention上实验，隐式偏置先验使商空间KL均值降低40.69%，PAC-Bayes证书均值降低21.40%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。纯理论工作，无实际部署考量，无遥感数据验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。如果非要联系，PAC-Bayes理论可用于分析轻量化模型的泛化界，但本文未涉及任何轻量化方法或遥感场景，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Overparameterized models often have continuous parameter symmetries, so different parameters define the same predictor. We show that PAC--Bayesian analysis should be performed on the quotient predictor space: pushing a prior and posterior to the quotient preserves the empirical and population Gibbs risks while removing the nonnegative KL contribution caused solely by how the two distributions differ among parameterizations of the same predictor.
  Quotienting alone does not determine which prior to use. We construct a canonical choice of one parameterization for each predictor and account for the geometric volume of its equivalent parameterizations. This transforms a neutral reference prior into a data-independent prior that reflects the model's implicit bias. It approximates the ideal but inadmissible posterior-matched prior, which would minimize the KL term by depending on the training data. The resulting certificate is tighter exactly when this geometry-induced prior has smaller KL divergence from the learned quotient posterior than the neutral prior.
  We test this prediction in Fourier regression with a Hadamard parameterization and in Query-Key attention, using ordinary SGD without an explicit regularizer. The implicit-bias prior reduces the mean quotient-space KL by \(40.69\%\) and the mean PAC--Bayes certificate by \(21.40\%\) in the Fourier-Hadamard experiment. The smaller, prior-scale-dependent improvement in Query-Key attention confirms the predicted conditional nature of the effect.

</details>


### [151] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E-SpecFormer，一种用于自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别的边缘频谱监测Transformer，核心贡献是LiTAN（无Softmax和LayerNorm的线性Tanh注意力机制），在FPGA/CPU上实现超低延迟（92μs/帧）和极小参数量（<10k）。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向的是射频（RF）信号处理中的调制识别和隐蔽信道识别，属于通信/频谱感知领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星影像、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心内容。虽然涉及边缘部署和轻量化，但场景和目标完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，属于RF信号处理领域。仅轻量注意力设计思路有微弱借鉴价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动调制识别（Automatic Modulation Recognition）和隐蔽信道识别（Covert Channel Recognition）；场景：物联网（IoT）设备上的实时频谱智能；传感器类型：射频信号（RF）；目标类别：调制方式和硬件木马隐蔽信道。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: E-SpecFormer（Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer），核心模块为LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network），一种去除了Softmax和LayerNorm的线性Tanh注意力机制。提供四种可扩展变体：Nano, Small, Medium, Large。

Lightweight method: 轻量注意力设计（LiTAN，无Softmax和LayerNorm）、多尺度变体（Nano/Small/Medium/Large）以适应不同硬件约束。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: FPGA/CPU协同执行，Nano变体参数量<10k，速度达92μs/帧。摘要未说明FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标。虽然部署效率极高，但面向的是RF边缘设备而非遥感卫星星上平台。

Datasets and metrics: RadioML2018数据集（调制识别），硬件木马（HT）隐蔽信道数据集；评价指标：平均准确率（Average Accuracy），SNR>0 dB条件下86.5%（调制识别），94.2%（隐蔽信道识别）。

Experiments: Nano变体在RadioML2018上SNR>0 dB达86.5%准确率，HT-CC数据集达94.2%准确率，参数量<10k，速度92μs/帧，超越SOTA边缘模型。摘要未说明与其他方法的详细对比和消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，无法直接迁移；2. 仅报告了准确率和延迟，缺乏FLOPs、模型大小（MB）、功耗等关键部署指标；3. 仅在RF信号数据集上验证，未在视觉/遥感任务上测试；4. 摘要未说明LiTAN注意力在视觉任务上的泛化能力。

Ideas for my research: 1. LiTAN（无Softmax和LayerNorm的线性Tanh注意力）的设计思路可借鉴到遥感轻量Transformer中，减少计算开销；2. 多尺度变体（Nano/Small/Medium/Large）的设计范式可用于遥感在轨模型的分级部署；3. FPGA/CPU协同推理的极低延迟（92μs）实现路径值得参考，但需验证在遥感视觉任务上的有效性；4. 参数量<10k的极端轻量级设计思路对星上资源受限场景有启发，但需重新设计适用于遥感图像的特征提取结构。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [152] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)，一种通过优化提示分布来引导贝叶斯滤波Transformer生成高期望效用续写的方法，无需对Transformer进行前向或反向传播。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是Transformer的贝叶斯后验行为建模与提示优化（prompt tuning），属于自然语言处理/生成领域的理论方法，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型行为引导（elicitation）、提示优化（prompt tuning）。应用场景：贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT）的续写生成。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT），基于latent posterior model的Transformer行为建模。PPT方法优化硬提示（hard prompt）的分布参数，使用predictive Monte Carlo (PMC)采样。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然PPT声称无需对Transformer进行前向/反向传播，但这属于推理阶段的提示优化策略，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 验证场景：Beta-Bernoulli和reinforced urn BFTs。效用函数（utility families）：reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity。不涉及遥感数据集或标准目标检测指标。

Experiments: 在三种效用函数族上验证PPT方法，但摘要未给出定量对比结果或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 方法仅在特定的BFT（贝叶斯滤波Transformer）上精确成立，泛化到普通Transformer的理论保证不明确。2. 与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。3. 摘要未报告计算开销、收敛速度等实际可用性指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：PPT中"无需对Transformer进行前向/反向传播"的思路，如果未来有工作将遥感目标检测模型视为某种"条件生成模型"，或许可借鉴其提示优化思想。但这一关联非常牵强，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

</details>


### [153] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种偏好条件化的强化学习公交信号优先控制器，通过运行时调节偏好参数w来权衡公交优先与整体交通延迟，无需重新训练。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是城市交通中的公交信号优先（TSP）问题，属于智能交通系统领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及任何遥感目标检测或轻量化部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：公交信号优先控制（Transit Signal Priority, TSP）；应用场景：城市交通路口信号灯控制；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：公交车、非公交车辆。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆检测、旋转框检测、小目标检测等）完全无关。

Model architecture: 偏好条件化强化学习控制器 π(a|s,w)，在IntersectionZoo框架上实现，引入约束信号控制/TSP封装。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在IntersectionZoo仿真环境中进行；评价指标包括：公交延迟、非公交交通延迟、尾延迟（tail-delay）、约束可行性等。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 与固定时间控制、基于规则的TSP覆盖、固定权重PPO专家进行对比。单个学习到的条件化策略在运行时偏好上覆盖平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于固定时间和基于规则的基线，并保持约束可行性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文聚焦城市交通信号控制，属于完全不同的研究领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的研究方向、任务场景、技术方法均无交集。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [154] [Deep learning-based prediction of time-resolved adhesive forces in viscoelastic Hertzian contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19060)
*Ali Maghami,Merten Stender,Michele Ciavarella,Antonio Papangelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究软粘弹性接触的快速力响应预测，使用LSTM等深度学习模型替代数值仿真，面向软体机器人抓取任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。其核心问题是软体机器人中的粘弹性接触力学预测，属于机器人/力学领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：力响应轨迹预测（sequence-to-sequence regression）；应用场景：软体机器人抓取与操控；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: LSTM网络（最佳架构）、TCN（时序卷积网络）、时序分布全连接层；采用scalar-conditioned conditioning机制（concatenated conditioning最佳）；引入固定测量步长（FMS）表示将变长轨迹转为定长序列。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提及模型推理时间中位数为0.16秒，但未讨论模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关内容。推理时间0.16秒/轨迹，但这是在地面GPU/CPU上还是特定硬件上未说明。无参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建仿真数据集，涵盖4个数量级的加载/卸载速率和不同驻留时间，Tabor参数范围0.2-3.2；评价指标：MSE（5.0×10⁻⁴）、中位拉脱力误差（≈2.2%）、中位滞后误差（≈1.1%）、推理时间（0.16s中位数）。

Experiments: 对比了LSTM、TCN、时序全连接三种架构及三种conditioning机制；最佳模型为concatenated conditioning的LSTM；在未见过的参数组合和分析极限情况下进行了测试。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术方法。该论文解决的是力学仿真加速问题，非视觉/检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于力学仿真代理模型（surrogate model）方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无交集。唯一的间接启发是：时序预测任务中LSTM对变长序列的处理方式（FMS表示）可能对遥感时序数据处理有参考价值，但与本课题核心目标（检测模型轻量化）无关。

Abstract: Fast prediction of the response of adhesive soft viscoelastic contacts represents a current challenge in soft robotics and for gripping and manipulation tasks. Determining the complete time-resolved force trajectory requires full numerical simulations, whose computational cost is strongly parameter-dependent, making them impractical for real-time application or design-optimization loops. In this work, we overcome this limitation by training a scalar-conditioned, stateful, sequence-to-sequence deep learning model to predict the full force evolution from a prescribed displacement history for both short- and long-range adhesion regimes. The data set spans four orders of magnitude in loading and unloading rates and includes varied dwell times, with the Tabor parameter ranging from $0.2$ to $3.2$. To enable learning across these heterogeneous time scales, we introduce a fixed-measurement-step (FMS) representation that converts variable-length trajectories into fixed-length sequences while preserving their physical-time information. Different architectures were trained, including long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, temporal convolutional neural (TCN) networks, and time-distributed dense layers with three different Tabor-conditioning mechanisms. The models were compared using global waveform and error metrics. We found that the best-performing model has an LSTM architecture with concatenated conditioning, which achieves a held-out mean-squared error of $5.0\times10^{-4}$, a median pull-off-force error of $\approx2.2\%$, and a median hysteresis error of $\approx1.1\%$. For the held-out protocols, the model predicts a complete force trajectory with a median inference time of $0.16$ s. The model is tested across unseen parameter combinations and against analytical limiting cases, providing a rapid surrogate for repeated numerical evaluations with potential use in control-oriented applications.

</details>


### [155] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BearingNAS硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，面向微控制器和智能传感器处理单元（ISPU）的极低资源（4-8KB RAM）在传感器内处理场景，在轴承故障诊断任务上达到99.50%准确率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。原因：(1) 应用场景是bearing fault diagnosis（轴承故障诊断），属于工业机械故障检测，与遥感/卫星/Earth observation无关；(2) 传感器类型是加速度计/IMU（惯性测量单元），非遥感卫星的光学/SAR/高光谱传感器；(3) 虽然涉及轻量化NAS和微控制器部署，但目标域完全不匹配，且未涉及目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，仅HW-NAS方法论有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：故障诊断（fault diagnosis），非目标检测；应用场景：工业轴承故障检测（bearing fault diagnosis）；传感器类型：加速度计/IMU（通过LSM6DSO16IS ISPU）；目标类别：轴承故障状态分类，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 搜索空间采用single data-flow search space + decaying kernel growth formulation（衰减核增长公式）防止参数爆炸；搜索策略为lightweight derivative-free search strategy（轻量无导数搜索策略）；目标硬件为STMicroelectronics的三款微控制器和LSM6DSO16IS ISPU。

Lightweight method: 核心方法是Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS)，面向极低资源预算（4-8KB RAM, 16-32KB Flash）；搜索在laptop CPU上运行，不到1小时收敛；无需离散GPU；属于NAS轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 目标硬件：STMicroelectronics微控制器和LSM6DSO16IS ISPU（智能传感器处理单元）；资源约束：4-8KB RAM, 16-32KB Flash；搜索在laptop CPU上完成；最终模型在ISPU上部署；摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark；评价指标：diagnostic accuracy（诊断准确率），最佳结果99.50%；摘要未说明其他指标。

Experiments: 在CWRU轴承基准上评估，针对三个STMicroelectronics目标硬件优化；最佳架构在ISPU上达到99.50%准确率；搜索在laptop CPU上不到1小时收敛；摘要未说明与SOTA方法的详细对比。

Limitations: (1) 应用领域完全为工业故障诊断，与遥感/卫星无关；(2) 任务为分类（故障诊断），非目标检测；(3) 传感器类型为加速度计，非遥感光学/SAR传感器；(4) 未涉及遥感常见的小目标、旋转框、多尺度等挑战；(5) 未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: (1) HW-NAS方法框架可借鉴：将搜索建模为约束优化问题，面向极低资源预算的思路可迁移到星上FPGA/NPU场景；(2) derivative-free search strategy + single data-flow search space的设计思路可用于遥感轻量模型搜索；(3) decaying kernel growth formulation防止参数爆炸的思想对遥感大模型压缩有参考价值；(4) 但需注意：该方法面向1D时序信号（加速度计），遥感图像是2D/3D数据，搜索空间设计需重新适配；(5) 该方法未涉及目标检测的bbox回归或旋转框，直接迁移需大量改造。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [156] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于DDIM的轻量级后验采样器PDDIM，用于求解线性逆问题，通过坐标级修改实现高效后验采样，并证明其收敛到贝叶斯后验分布。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于扩散模型求解线性逆问题的后验采样算法，属于生成模型/图像恢复领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：线性逆问题求解（图像恢复/重建）；应用场景：通用图像恢复（如去噪、超分、修复等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于DDIM（Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models）的变体PDDIM，核心是沿测量算子奇异方向进行坐标级后验采样，根据观测SNR与扩散SNR的比较切换先验/测量驱动预测。

Lightweight method: 论文自称"lightweight"（轻量级），但这里的轻量是指算法实现简单（仅需对标准DDIM更新做坐标级修改），并非模型压缩意义上的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。不涉及星上/边缘部署相关的轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然算法本身计算开销可能低于其他扩散后验采样器，但扩散模型整体推理成本仍然较高，不适合星上实时推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为"the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered"，但未列出具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在多种图像恢复任务上与现有扩散后验采样器对比，PDDIM在大多数评价指标上取得最优结果。但摘要未给出定量对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 扩散模型推理速度慢，不适合在轨实时检测；3. 仅针对线性逆问题，不涉及目标检测任务；4. 摘要未说明在遥感图像上的实验验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 扩散模型在图像恢复/超分方面的能力可用于遥感图像预处理增强，但需考虑星上计算资源限制；2. 其"坐标级修改"的轻量设计思路（在现有框架上做最小改动实现新功能）在模型适配时有一定参考价值，但与轻量化核心课题无关。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两时间尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），用于优化边缘-云分层计算系统中的服务放置、计算委派和功率控制，以最小化端到端延迟。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是通用边缘-云分层计算系统中的负载均衡和延迟优化问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"边缘计算"与星上部署有概念上的弱关联，但论文核心是通信与计算资源调度，而非视觉模型压缩或遥感图像处理。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：边缘-云分层计算系统中的服务放置、计算委派与功率控制联合优化；应用场景：通用边缘计算网络；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 两时间尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），包含长期系统配置子问题和短期资源分配子问题；使用变分自编码器（VAE）对高维组合动作空间进行潜在动作表示压缩。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"轻量化"仅体现在使用VAE对动作空间进行压缩（latent action representation），属于强化学习中的动作空间降维，与模型剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等深度学习模型轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、延迟等与星上部署直接相关的指标。文中提到的"延迟"是端到端通信与计算延迟，非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真实验，未使用遥感或视觉数据集；评价指标：平均端到端延迟（average e2e latency）、资源利用率（resource utilization）、收敛速度。

Experiments: 与branch-and-bound最优解对比达到近最优性能；相比无计算委派的方案，平均端到端延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何视觉模型或图像处理；3. 未讨论模型轻量化或星上部署约束；4. 仅在仿真环境中验证，未在真实硬件上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果未来研究方向涉及"星上-地面协同推理"中的任务卸载决策，可参考其两时间尺度优化思路，但与本课题核心的"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"无直接关联。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [158] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向表格异常检测的持续学习基准场景设计框架，用于从现有数据集中构建可复现的持续异常检测任务序列。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于表格数据的持续异常检测（Continual Anomaly Detection），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：持续异常检测（Continual Anomaly Detection, CAD）；应用场景：网络安全（cybersecurity）表格数据；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：异常检测，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构，仅讨论基准场景设计框架。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标。

Experiments: 生成了5个基准场景（benchmark-ready scenarios），包含单数据集和多数据集持续异常检测设置。具体实验结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文仅针对表格数据的持续异常检测基准设计问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。持续学习（Continual Learning）概念本身在遥感在轨场景中可能有间接参考价值（如模型在轨适应新类别），但本文聚焦表格异常检测且未涉及遥感或目标检测，借鉴意义极低。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [159] [Multi-layer MIMO Relay as Deep Physical Neural Networks: Power Amplifiers as Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18354)
*Meng Hua,Itsik Bergel,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无线物理神经网络（WPNN），利用多跳MIMO中继网络的硬件非线性实现模拟域深度神经网络，用于图像分类。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线物理神经网络（WPNN）的模拟硬件实现，属于通信与模拟计算交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（image classification）；应用场景：无线模拟神经网络推理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 深度WPNN，基于多跳MIMO中继网络，每跳中继实现可训练的复数线性增益和偏置，利用功率放大器固有非线性作为激活函数，构成空口全连接网络。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何模型压缩或加速方法。其低能耗优势来源于模拟硬件本身，而非模型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅定性提及比传统数字实现能耗和延迟更低，但未给出量化数据，且与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及"simulation results"和"image classification"。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证了所提架构在图像分类上的推理准确性，并对比了LS-based和SVD-based两种收发方案。消融实验等细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对图像分类任务，不涉及目标检测；3. 未讨论模型轻量化方法；4. 缺乏实际部署的量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；5. 模拟硬件的实际精度和鲁棒性有待验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发点是：模拟计算/存算一体架构在极端低功耗场景下的潜力，但这属于硬件层面的方向，与"模型轻量化"（算法层面）不同。对于遥感卫星在轨目标检测课题，本论文无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Wireless physical neural networks (WPNNs) embed neural computation directly into analog hardware, offering lower energy consumption and latency than conventional digital implementations. In this paper, we propose a deep WPNN in which nonlinear activations are realized by a multi-hop multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) relay network, in which each relay implements a trainable complex linear gain and bias, followed by the power amplifier's intrinsic nonlinearity acting as an activation function. The cascade of multiple relays therefore realizes an over-the-air fully connected network whose parameters can be trained end-to-end. We develop two transceiver designs for different channel state information (CSI) availability scenarios: a least squares (LS)-based scheme requiring only receiver-side CSI, and a singular-value-decomposition (SVD)-based scheme requiring both transmitter-side and receiver-side CSI. Simulation results show that the proposed architecture enables accurate over-the-air inference for image classification. In particular, the results highlight the advantage of exploiting hardware nonlinearity for enhanced inference capability.

</details>


### [160] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双曲正割(sech)函数的KAN架构SechKAN，在函数拟合、PDE和图像分类任务上优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数量与MLP相当但推理时间略长。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于通用神经网络架构设计（KAN变体），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感场景；不涉及目标检测（仅做图像分类）；不涉及轻量化（虽然参数量与MLP相当，但未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等轻量化技术）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：函数拟合、PDE求解、图像分类；应用场景：通用机器学习/科学计算；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：MNIST手写数字、Fashion-MNIST服饰、CIFAR-10/100自然图像。

Model architecture: SechKAN：基于Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs)架构，使用双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数，采用1D线性变换减少参数量。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及轻量化方法。仅提到使用1D线性变换使参数量与MLP相当，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术，也未讨论推理加速或部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量具体数值、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到运行时间优于其他KAN变体但略长于MLP，未讨论任何星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100（均为通用图像分类基准）；评价指标：分类准确率（具体数值摘要未说明）；还涉及函数拟合和PDE问题的实验（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在函数拟合、PDE问题、图像分类三个任务上实验，SechKAN优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数量与MLP相当。运行时间优于其他KAN变体但略慢于MLP。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，未在遥感数据集上验证；2. 仅做图像分类，不涉及目标检测；3. 推理速度慢于MLP，不利于实时/在轨部署；4. 未讨论任何模型压缩或边缘部署技术；5. 仅在小型通用数据集上验证，未在大规模遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。如果未来有工作将KAN引入遥感目标检测，SechKAN的sech基函数和1D线性变换降参策略可作为参考，但当前论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联性极低。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [161] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种双域融合LSTM模型(DDF-LSTM)，用于工程系统时变可靠性分析，通过将时不变变量嵌入LSTM初始隐状态并设计改进损失函数，提升失效概率估计精度与计算效率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于工程系统的时变可靠性分析（time-dependent reliability analysis），属于结构可靠性/机械工程领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测（object detection）或轻量化（lightweight）方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时变可靠性分析（time-dependent reliability analysis）；应用场景：工程系统长期安全评估；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DDF-LSTM（Dual-Domain Fused LSTM），将时不变变量嵌入LSTM初始隐状态，全连接层映射LSTM输出与时不变变量到最终输出空间。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"minimal computational cost"用于蒙特卡洛仿真，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 四个案例研究（four case studies）验证方法，但摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 通过四个案例验证了计算效率和预测精度提升，但未给出具体对比方法和消融实验结果。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性；与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，LSTM结构在遥感目标检测中已被CNN/Transformer替代，且未涉及任何轻量化设计。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的方向是：如果未来研究涉及遥感时序数据（如时序变化检测），其双域融合思路（时变+时不变变量联合建模）可能有参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向不匹配。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [162] [A Better Start for Language Models: Domain-Conditional Position Offsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18302)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种域条件位置偏移（domain-conditional position offset）方法，通过在序列起始位置添加一个可学习向量来缓解自回归语言模型的冷启动惩罚，无需修改模型权重，训练速度快、可热切换、无延迟开销。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自然语言处理中的自回归语言模型冷启动问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自回归语言模型序列起始位置的冷启动惩罚缓解；应用场景：NLP领域的域自适应文本生成与评分；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 在Mamba、GPT-NeoX、Llama等自回归语言模型（410M-8B参数，延伸至70B）的embedding层添加一个可学习的域条件位置偏移向量，模型权重保持冻结。

Lightweight method: 方法本身是一种轻量级适配手段：仅需训练一个向量（约数分钟，约100个文档），不增加推理延迟，可热切换域。但这是NLP领域的轻量适配，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等视觉模型轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的内容。无参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：约100个文档的域内数据；评价指标：held-out in-domain perplexity（困惑度），检索重排序和域分类准确率。

Experiments: 在8个模型（Mamba, GPT-NeoX, Llama, 410M-8B参数，延伸至70B）上验证，域内困惑度降低最高27%；与LoRA对比（LoRA参数多2-3个数量级但困惑度更低）；与直接logit偏置校正对比（最多7.9%）；消融实验表明单个位置即可获得大部分收益。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 方法针对NLP序列建模的冷启动问题，无法直接迁移到视觉目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但"在冻结模型上添加少量可学习参数实现域自适应"的思想，理论上可启发遥感目标检测中的域自适应轻量化方法（如不同卫星传感器间的域偏移校准），但需要大幅改造才能适用于视觉任务。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are least accurate at the beginning of a sequence, where little context forces reliance on a generic pretraining prior. We show that this cold-start penalty is domain dependent and reduce it with a domain-conditional position offset: a single learned vector added to the embedding activation at the first sequence positions while all model weights remain frozen. The offset trains in minutes on roughly one hundred documents, switches between domains without added sequence state, and has no measurable latency overhead. Across eight Mamba, GPT-NeoX, and Llama models spanning 410M to 8B parameters, it reduces held-out in-domain perplexity by up to 27%; the effect persists at 70B, and one position captures most of the benefit. A matched, converged direct logit-bias correction reaches at most only 7.9% and leaves later-token loss unchanged, showing that the offset propagates through model state rather than merely recalibrating the output prior. A tuned LoRA reaches lower perplexity but uses two to three orders of magnitude more parameters and an active low-rank weight path, while soft prompts add sequence positions. With wrong-domain controls, offsets improve retrieval reranking and domain classification when decisions depend on early in-domain tokens, For the few-shot reasoning whose signal occurs later, the results maintains unchanged. Position-aware prefill application also help generation tasks, whereas naive application at every cached decoding step causes repetition. The offset is therefore not the strongest adapter, but a lightweight, hot switchable tool for short in-domain scoring and calibration.

</details>


### [163] [TD-DPO: Difference-Aware Preference Optimization for Mitigating Sycophancy in Clinical Autism Intervention Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18304)
*Shuzhong Lai,Junhong Lai,Chenxi Li,Qing Zhou,Haifeng Li,Gang Pan,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDA数据增强策略和TD-DPO优化方法，用于减少大语言模型在自闭症儿童干预对话中的谄媚行为，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是大语言模型在自闭症干预对话中的谄媚行为缓解问题，属于NLP/对齐领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型对齐（sycophancy mitigation）；场景：自闭症儿童干预对话；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的序列级/Token级偏好优化，具体骨干网络摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为sycophancy mitigation效果与intervention ability retention的trade-off。

Experiments: 在多个backbone和evaluator上进行了实验，TD-DPO在离线设置下实现了更好的谄媚缓解与干预能力保持的权衡。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无。该论文研究的是LLM对齐问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化，对本课题无借鉴意义。

Abstract: The sycophancy of large language models can increase the safety risk in intervention dialogue for autistic children. Supervised fine-tuning can somewhat reduce sycophancy, but relying solely on positive examples is often insufficient to identify and correct failure patterns. We observe that sycophancy behaviors can often be localized to a limited span within the model response. In this regime, sequence-level preference optimization can over-update preference-irrelevant tokens and degrade intervention ability. To address this, we propose the \textbf{M}inimal \textbf{E}dit \textbf{D}ata \textbf{A}ugmentation (MEDA) strategy to construct controlled, stable, minimal edit preference pairs and \textbf{T}oken-level \textbf{D}ifference \textbf{D}irect \textbf{P}reference \textbf{O}ptimization (TD-DPO), which upweights difference tokens between chosen and rejected responses while downweighting shared tokens to suppress background drift. Extensive experiments across multiple backbones and evaluators show that TD-DPO achieves a better trade-off between sycophancy mitigation and intervention ability retention in our offline settings, highlighting its potential as a practical alignment approach for autism intervention.

</details>


### [164] [Agentic Calibration of Grey-Box Simulation Models: An LLM-Driven Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18308)
*David Gómez-Guillén,Mireia Diaz,Josep Lluis Arcos,Jesús Cerquides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）作为优化器的智能体校准方法，用于灰盒仿真模型的约束优化，在较少模型评估次数下达到与贝叶斯优化相当或更优的误差水平。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于仿真模型（anal cancer simulation model）的参数校准优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心要素。虽然"轻量化"在方法论层面（减少模型评估次数）有抽象类比可能，但场景、任务、数据、模型类型均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：灰盒仿真模型的参数校准（constrained optimization）；场景：医学仿真（anal cancer simulation model）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 使用大语言模型（LLM）作为优化器（agentic optimizer），通过系统提示（system prompt）以自然语言形式嵌入约束条件。对比方法包括Nelder-Mead (NM)和贝叶斯优化（Bayesian Optimization, BO）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是减少仿真模型评估次数（sample efficiency），而非神经网络模型的剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。相反，论文指出LLM作为优化器的主要代价是每次迭代的推理时间增加（increased inference time per iteration），这与星上部署的低延迟、低功耗需求相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：anal cancer simulation model（仿真模型，非真实遥感数据集）；评价指标：best error（最佳误差）、model evaluations（模型评估次数）、inference time per iteration（每次迭代推理时间）。

Experiments: 在无约束和有约束两种设置下对比Agentic方法、NM和BO。无约束下Agentic方法以更少评估次数达到更低误差；有约束下Agentic与BO误差相当，均优于NM。代价是每次迭代推理时间增加。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. LLM作为优化器每次迭代推理成本高，不适合资源受限的星上场景；3. 仅在一个医学仿真模型上验证，泛化性未知；4. 未讨论任何视觉/检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的抽象启发是：在模型轻量化搜索（如NAS、剪枝率搜索）中，是否可以用LLM作为搜索策略的优化器来替代传统搜索算法，但这属于非常间接且高成本的思路，实际可行性存疑。

Abstract: Calibration of grey-box simulation models is a constrained optimization problem in which model evaluations are expensive, the parameter space can be high-dimensional, and the search must respect plausibility constraints. Although the simulation code is fully available to the analyst, the joint effect of multiple parameters remains difficult to predict analytically. Classical optimizers such as Nelder--Mead (NM) are simple to deploy but sample-inefficient, particularly under constraints. Modern Bayesian Optimization methods achieve competitive solutions with far fewer evaluations but require non-trivial modeling machinery for constraint handling. We introduce an agentic calibration method in which a large language model acts as the optimizer, with constraints incorporated as a plain-language section of the system prompt. We evaluate the agentic method, NM, and Bayesian Optimization (BO) on an anal cancer simulation model under both unconstrained and clinically constrained calibration. Under unconstrained calibration, the agentic method achieves substantially lower best error than BO and NM, while requiring fewer model evaluations. Under constrained calibration, the agentic method reaches comparable error levels and both outperform NM. These results are obtained at the cost of increased inference time per iteration. Agentic calibration achieves competitive performance with substantially fewer model evaluations, and constraint handling is essentially free at the modeller-facing interface through simple textual specifications rather than additional modelling machinery. The main trade-off lies in increased per-iteration inference cost, making the approach particularly suitable when simulation time dominates. Beyond performance, the per-iteration rationale makes the search auditable and explainable, so its decisions can be scrutinised and justified to third parties.

</details>


### [165] [Spatio-Temporal Prediction of Unsteady Airfoil Aerodynamics Using Augmented Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations with Exogenous Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18309)
*Henrik Lange,Reik Thormann,Philipp Bekemeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GNODE（图神经常微分方程）方法，用于非定常气动力的时空预测，以替代传统CFD方法。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。研究的是航空飞行器气动力学中的非定常流场预测，属于计算流体力学（CFD）与机器学习结合的交叉方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：非定常气动力时空预测（spatio-temporal prediction of unsteady aerodynamic forces）；场景：航空飞行器气动设计与优化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用（预测对象为俯仰翼型的表面力）。

Model architecture: GNODE = Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) + Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (Neural ODEs)；采用自回归方式在离散空间域上进行时空预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及ML方法作为surrogate model比传统CFD计算更快，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：俯仰翼型仿真数据集（包含跨音速激波、瞬态行为、动态非线性）；评价指标：时间稳定性（temporal stability）、空间平滑性（spatial smoothness）、预测精度（accuracy）。

Experiments: 与自回归GNN基线对比，GNODE在时间稳定性、空间平滑性和精度上均更优；增加潜在维度可提升表达力和精度。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在俯仰翼型仿真数据上验证，未在真实遥感数据上测试；3. 未讨论模型大小、推理速度、边缘部署等实际工程问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于流体力学+图神经网络的交叉方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究目标、任务场景、技术路线均不相关。

Abstract: Unsteady aerodynamic phenomena, such as gusts, turbulence, and fluid-structure interactions affect an aircraft during flight. For design, optimisation and certification, it is indispensable to quantify such unsteady aerodynamic effects. Industry-standard computational fluid dynamics methods, such as solving the unsteady Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes equations or the linearized frequency domain method, are either computationally expensive or restricted by assumptions like linearity. Once trained, machine learning methods are capable of computing non-linear relationships very fast, making them suitable as surrogate models. By autoregressively applying graph neural networks (GNNs), operating on a discretised spatial domain, spatio-temporal predictions can be made. However, autoregressive GNNs suffer from error accumulation leading to unstable rollouts over time. Here we show that combining GNNs with augmented Neural Ordinary Differential Equations yields temporally stable predictions of the surface forces on a pitching airfoil. We found that our approach, called GNODE, based on Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations, provides temporally more stable, spatially smoother, and overall more accurate results than an autoregressive GNN baseline. Tests are conducted on a dataset consisting of a simulations of a pitching airfoil, including transonic shocks, transient behaviour and dynamic non-linearities. Augmenting GNODEs with additional latent dimensions improves the expressivity and accuracy by capturing underlying history effects. The developed method demonstrates an approach that is suitable to model non-linear spatio-temporal systems with exogenous inputs.

</details>


### [166] [Interactive Training 2: Auditable Control Plane for Live Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18314)
*Wentao Zhang,Xuanhe Pan,Han Zhou,Yang Lu,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Interactive Training 2，一个用于训练过程交互式控制的开放协议和系统，支持人类和自动化控制器在训练过程中动态调整参数。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于训练过程的交互式控制协议和工具，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。
