Datasets and metrics: 使用了三类任务的数据集：生物分子系统的粗粒化到原子化反向映射数据、分子固体的NMR化学位移数据、以及受蛋白质二级结构基序启发的合成基准数据。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提到"competitive accuracy"和"high fidelity"。

Experiments: 在三类不同任务上验证了框架的灵活性，展示了通过配置切换即可在不同任务上达到有竞争力的精度。在合成基准上验证了GEqDiff对具有异构变换属性的场进行联合重建的能力。摘要未提供与SOTA方法的详细定量对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 作者明确表示目标不是超越针对特定任务优化的系统，而是展示共享框架的灵活性，因此精度可能不是最优；3. 摘要未报告计算效率指标；4. 仅验证了分子科学领域的任务，泛化到其他领域（如遥感）未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能值得注意的点是：该框架通过配置驱动的方式实现了模型在不同任务间的复用，这种"配置分离"的设计思想在遥感目标检测模型的多任务适配（如同时检测舰船、飞机、车辆）中可能有启发，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化、在轨部署）关联极弱。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks provide a powerful modeling language for three-dimensional scientific data, but their reuse is often limited by implementations tied to specific tasks, outputs, and training regimes. We present GEqTrain, a configuration-driven framework that separates dataset semantics, model composition, and training objectives. Raw data are mapped to typed node-, edge-, and graph-level fields, while model stacks, losses, and training workflows are assembled declaratively through Hydra configurations. A shared equivariant backbone and training infrastructure can therefore be retargeted to a new task primarily through configuration. We demonstrate this flexibility on three different problems handled within one software stack: coarse-grained-to-atomistic backmapping of biomolecular systems, prediction of NMR chemical shifts in molecular solids, and equivariant generative modeling. Our aim is not to surpass individually optimized task-specific systems, but to show that a shared representation and training infrastructure can achieve competitive accuracy across qualitatively different tasks at the cost of a configuration change. We further introduce GEqDiff, a generative extension based on equivariant flow matching. GEqDiff treats user-defined equivariant fields as first-class generation targets, jointly transporting Cartesian positions and non-scalar node fields spanning representations up to l=3 within a single equivariant flow. We validate this capability on a controlled synthetic benchmark inspired by protein secondary-structure motifs, showing that fields with heterogeneous transformation properties can be reconstructed jointly and with high fidelity. By reducing the software overhead of moving between predictive and generative, scalar and tensorial settings, GEqTrain aims to make equivariant modeling more reproducible, extensible, and reusable.

</details>


### [218] [An unsupervised clustering analysis of breast cancer data derived from electronic health records enhanced through UMAP dimensionality reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19089)
*Davide Chicco,Nicoletta Benvenuto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于UMAP降维+DBSCAN聚类的乳腺癌患者电子健康记录分组方法，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文主题为乳腺癌患者电子健康记录的聚类分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无监督聚类（DBSCAN）；应用场景：乳腺癌患者电子健康记录分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DBSCAN密度聚类 + UMAP降维，非深度学习模型，无神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。UMAP降维仅用于数据可视化/预处理，并非模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 三个独立的乳腺癌患者电子健康记录数据集；聚类评价指标：DBCV、DCSI、DISCO。

Experiments: UMAP+DBSCAN组合在三个数据集上的聚类效果优于单独使用DBSCAN，通过三个统计指标验证。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医学信息学/生物信息学领域的无监督聚类分析，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、数据、方法、评价指标上均无交集。

Abstract: Breast cancer is one of the most widespread types of cancer, affecting approximately 8 million women worldwide. Electronic health records of patients diagnosed with this disease can serve as valuable datasets for computational analyses, enabling the discovery of new insights about the pathology. Unsupervised clustering, in particular, can identify groups of patients with medically significant features, revealing data trends that might otherwise go unnoticed by medical doctors. In this study, we first applied the DBSCAN density-based clustering method to three independent datasets derived from electronic medical records of patients with mammary carcinoma. Subsequently, to enhance our results, we preceded the DBSCAN application with a dimensionality reduction phase using UMAP. We evaluated our clustering outcomes using three statistical indices (DBCV, DCSI, and DISCO). Our results confirm the effectiveness of combining UMAP with DBSCAN for clustering data derived from electronic health records, paving the way for the medical interpretation of the patient groups identified by our approach.

</details>


### [219] [Predicting Activities in Aqueous Electrolyte Solutions with Hybrid Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19114)
*Zeno Romero,Maximilian Kohns,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将Bromley物理模型与矩阵补全机器学习方法相结合的混合模型，用于预测水溶液中电解质的活度系数和渗透系数，属于电化学/热力学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是水溶液电解质活度系数的预测问题，属于物理化学/热力学领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。没有任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：电解质溶液活度系数和渗透系数的预测；场景：工业与自然界中的水溶液建模；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 混合模型：Bromley物理模型 + 矩阵补全方法(MCM)。Bromley模型提供物理约束，MCM用于预测Bromley参数矩阵中缺失的元素。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Dortmund Data Bank中478种电解质在298K下的平均离子活度系数和渗透系数实验数据；评价指标：预测精度（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 训练方式：端到端训练；验证方式：对未参与训练的电解质进行预测评估，展示了模型的外推能力；具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 仅针对298K温度，温度泛化性未讨论；3. 矩阵补全方法对稀疏数据的处理能力可能受限于数据分布。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究内容与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Activities in aqueous electrolyte solutions, usually described by ionic activity and osmotic coefficients, are important properties for modeling many processes in industry and nature. Established activity models, such as those of Pitzer or Bromley, require fitting to experimental data for each electrolyte of interest and thus cannot predict properties for unstudied systems. While some predictive approaches exist, they are typically limited in scope and rely on additional ion-specific descriptors. In this work, we introduce a new hybrid model that combines the physics-based Bromley model with a matrix completion method (MCM) from machine learning. The MCM is employed to predict the electrolyte-specific parameters of the Bromley model, exploiting the fact that these parameters can be arranged in a matrix with cations and anions as rows and columns, respectively. Due to the lack of experimental data for many electrolytes, the initial parameter matrix is sparsely populated, making the prediction of the Bromley parameters for unstudied electrolytes a matrix completion problem. The hybrid model, Bromley-MCM, was trained end-to-end on experimental data for mean ionic activity coefficients and osmotic coefficients of aqueous solutions of 478 electrolytes at 298 K from the Dortmund Data Bank. As output, we obtain a completed matrix of Bromley parameters for 83 cations and 112 anions, enabling consistent prediction of concentration-dependent activities in aqueous solutions of 9,296 electrolytes at 298~K. This substantially extends the applicability of the Bromley model while maintaining high predictive accuracy, as demonstrated through evaluations on electrolytes excluded from model training.

</details>


### [220] [Parallel Noising in Neural Markov Logic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19126)
*Peter Jung,Giuseppe Marra,Ondrej Kuzelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文增强神经马尔可夫逻辑网络(NMLNs)在图生成任务上的表现，通过图神经网络增强势函数和并行噪声训练算法，使其在分子结构生成等任务上匹配扩散模型和文本基循环模型。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图生成（graph generation）和神经符号模型（neurosymbolic model），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图生成（graph generation）；应用场景：分子结构生成（small molecular structures）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Neural Markov Logic Networks (NMLNs)，使用图神经网络（GNN）增强势函数的表达能力。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集和评价指标。提及与"diffusion-based generative graph models"和"specialized text-based recurrent models"对比。

Experiments: 实验表明：增强后的NMLNs在图生成任务上达到与通用扩散图生成模型相当的性能，并在小分子结构生成任务上匹配专用文本基循环模型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于图生成与神经符号学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Neural Markov Logic Networks (NMLNs) are a flexible neurosymbolic relational model. Previous work has shown that, although NMLNs achieve strong performance as generative models for small relational structures, they underperform diffusion-based generative graph models on larger structures. In this paper, we strengthen NMLNs along two main dimensions: (i) we increase the expressive capacity of their potential functions using graph neural networks, and (ii) we develop a new training and inference algorithm inspired by parallel-tempering Markov chain Monte Carlo methods, which we name parallel noising. Together, these enhancements enable NMLNs to attain strong performance in graph generation relative to general diffusion-based generative graph models. Furthermore, they allow NMLNs to match the performance of specialized text-based recurrent models when generating small molecular structures.

</details>


### [221] [One Model, Many Graphs: Learning over Attributed Graphs across Heterogeneous Modalities with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19128)
*Jiayi Yang,Yifang Chen,Yuanfu Sun,Jiajin Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OMG-VLM框架，利用预训练视觉-语言模型作为统一骨干网络，处理不同模态属性（文本、图像、多模态）的图结构数据，实现跨模态图学习的统一建模。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于图结构数据上的多模态学习（文本/图像属性图），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图学习（节点分类、链接预测）；场景：通用图结构数据（非遥感场景）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于预训练VLM（视觉-语言模型）作为共享骨干网络，引入结构感知图适配器（structure-aware graph adapters）以整合邻域信息，保持与VLM原生嵌入空间的兼容性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确列出具体数据集名称和指标，仅提及在多种图学习任务（节点分类、链接预测）上进行了广泛实验。

Experiments: OMG-VLM在多种图学习任务上一致优于基于GNN和LLM的基线方法，并展现出对未见图和新模态模式的强泛化能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。该工作面向通用图学习，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测任务、实时推理或模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发点：VLM作为统一骨干的思想或许可迁移到遥感多模态（光学+SAR+文本标注）联合表征，但与本课题的轻量化目标检测核心方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a unified representation space for textual and visual information, yet their potential as general-purpose backbones for graph-structured data remains largely unexplored. In practice, attributed graphs exhibit substantial modality heterogeneity: some graphs contain only textual node attributes, others only visual attributes, while still others provide both. Existing graph learning approaches are typically designed for fixed modality schemas, requiring separate models for different settings and limiting scalability and cross-graph generalization. To bridge this gap, we present OMG-VLM (One Model, Many Graphs with Vision-Language Models), a unified framework for learning over attributed graphs across heterogeneous modality schemas. OMG-VLM leverages a pretrained VLM as a shared backbone and introduces structure-aware graph adapters that integrate neighborhood information while remaining compatible with the VLM's native embedding space. This design enables effective learning over text-attributed, image-attributed, and multi-attributed graphs within a single model. Extensive experiments across diverse domains show that OMG-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GNN- and LLM-based baselines on attributed graph learning tasks such as node classification and link prediction, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen graphs and varying modality schemas. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jo-eyang/OMG-VLM.

</details>


### [222] [Incomplete Observations Boost Evolutionary Performance in Ocean Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19147)
*Yangyang Kong,Yutong Jiang,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong,Feng Gao,Xiaopei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式状态空间模型，直接从稀疏含噪观测数据学习海洋状态演化，用于海洋场重建与预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文涉及卫星数据（FY-3D），但核心任务是海洋状态重建与预测（ocean modeling），而非遥感图像目标检测；不涉及任何目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：海洋状态重建与预测（ocean field reconstruction & prediction）；场景：海洋建模；传感器类型：卫星观测数据（FY-3D）、CMIP6模拟数据；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 生成式状态空间模型（generative state-space model），本质是连续状态空间的隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）；初始状态模块和状态转移模块用神经网络实现；发射模块为掩码高斯分布（masked Gaussian distribution）；训练采用基于EM算法的优化框架，结合Langevin dynamics重建高保真海洋场。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到效率方面假设状态演化是平稳遍历马尔可夫过程，并采用长度-2的状态序列进行优化以降低计算开销。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CMIP6模拟数据、FY-3D卫星数据；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提及高保真重建（high-fidelity reconstruction）和准确预测（accurate prediction）。

Experiments: 在CMIP6和FY-3D数据上验证了高保真重建和准确预测能力，表明稀疏观测可直接改善模型对海洋状态动力学的表征。未提供与其他方法的定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论；3. 实验缺乏定量指标和对比基线；4. 仅适用于海洋状态建模，无法迁移至目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可参考的点是使用卫星观测数据（FY-3D）进行模型训练，但任务类型（重建/预测 vs. 检测）完全不同，方法（状态空间模型 vs. CNN/Transformer检测器）也无交集。

Abstract: Data-driven methods have revolutionized ocean modeling, yet current approaches rely heavily on complete reanalysis datasets, imposing computational constraints and limiting model performance to that of the training data. Here, we present a generative state-space model and an optimization framework that enable learning directly from sparse and noisy observations. The model is essentially a hidden Markov model with a continuous state space, where oceanic physical quantities are treated as hidden states and measurements as observations, enabling a unified representation of ocean fields and observational data. Both the initial-state and state-transition modules are implemented as neural networks to capture the complexity and temporal evolution of ocean states, while the emission module is formulated as a masked Gaussian distribution. To train the model from sparse observations, we derive an optimization framework based on the expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm. The framework alternately reconstructs high-fidelity ocean fields via Langevin dynamics and optimizes deep neural networks to capture temporal evolution. Theoretical analysis shows that the framework maximizes the likelihood of observations under the generative model. For efficiency, we assume that ocean-state evolution follows a stationary, ergodic, and Markovian stochastic process and adopt only length-two state sequences during optimization. Experiments on CMIP6 simulation data and FY-3D satellite data demonstrate high-fidelity reconstruction and accurate prediction, showing that sparse observations can directly improve the model's representation of ocean-state dynamics. This work offers a scalable pathway for next-generation Earth system models to learn directly from sparse, incomplete real-world observations.

</details>


### [223] [Breaking the Homogeneity Assumption: Specialized Multi-Generator Adversarial Learning for Rare Failure Detection in Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19153)
*Alexis Lazanas,Georgios Kampouropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向预测性维护中非均衡工业故障数据的多生成器GAN增强方法，用于改善少数类故障的识别性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于工业预测性维护（predictive maintenance）中的非均衡数据增强问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：工业故障分类（非目标检测）；场景：预测性维护；传感器类型：工业传感器；目标类别：机器故障子类型。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 多生成器GAN架构（multi-generator GAN），每个生成器独立学习一个故障子类型的分布。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance dataset（工业预测性维护数据集，非遥感数据集）；评价指标：PR-AUC（Precision-Recall AUC）、Recall。

Experiments: 对比方法：cost-sensitive learning、random undersampling、SMOTE oversampling、single-generator GAN augmentation；消融实验：多生成器 vs 单生成器；结果：多生成器GAN在PR-AUC和Recall上优于传统重采样方法和单生成器GAN。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 仅在单一工业数据集上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：多生成器GAN处理多模态少数类分布的思想，理论上可迁移到遥感小目标检测中的类别不均衡问题（如罕见舰船/飞机类型），但本文方法本身不涉及检测任务，且GAN生成遥感图像的真实性和可控性仍需额外验证。

Abstract: Supervised learning models in the predictive maintenance field are regularly trained on highly imbalanced industrial datasets: machine failures occur rarely but have a disproportionate effect on operations. In addition to the clear class disparity, failure data are typically non-homogeneous, with different failure modes arising from distinct physical processes and exhibiting a multimodal distribution across minorities and classes. Traditional imbalance-management methods, e.g., undersampling, SMOTE-based interpolation, or cost-sensitive learning, typically assume that the minority population is homogeneous. This means their effectiveness is severely limited in the multifaceted conditions encountered in industrial practice. This paper determines the possibility of a failure-type-conscious generative augmentation program to improve the identification of infrequent failures in predictive maintenance systems. An experimental design that is leakage-safe is used to compare five imbalance-handling methods: cost-sensitive learning, random undersampling, SMOTE oversampling, single-generator GAN augmentation, and a specialized multi-generator GAN architecture that has independent generators that are asked to learn individual failure subtypes. Precision/Recall-oriented measures are used to quantify model performance; the main evaluation measure is the PR-AUC. Experiments conducted on the AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance dataset indicate that the proposed multi-generator GAN framework produces more realistic minority samples, yielding higher PR-AUC and recall scores compared to traditional resampling methods and individual-generator GAN augmentation.

</details>


### [224] [Neural Kolmogorov Equations: Parallelizable Learning of Stochastic Dynamics under General Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19173)
*Arthur Bizzi,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经Kolmogorov方程(NKEs)，用确定性无穷维框架替代神经SDE，通过Kolmogorov前向方程学习Lévy型随机动力学，实现时间并行训练。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于随机微分方程的神经求解方法，属于科学计算/物理信息机器学习方向，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为随机动力学建模与概率密度演化，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于Kolmogorov前向方程(KFE)的确定性无穷维神经网络框架，采用Lagrangian Galerkin投影和算子分裂实现时间并行训练。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。在包含耦合噪声和跳跃过程的随机基准系统上评估，使用预测精度和训练效率作为指标。

Experiments: 在多个随机基准上验证，包括耦合噪声和跳跃过程系统，声称能准确恢复确定性和随机动力学，预测精度有竞争力，训练效率提升。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。摘要未说明在遥感数据上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于科学计算/随机过程建模领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning noisy or stochastic dynamics directly from data; however, existing approaches largely assume uncoupled and continuous noise, limiting their applicability to realistic stochastic drivers, and often scale poorly in time, requiring expensive autoregressive training. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Kolmogorov Equations (NKEs), a deterministic, infinite-dimensional reformulation of Neural SDEs based on the Kolmogorov Forward equation, transforming the learning problem from modelling individual stochastic trajectories to modelling the evolution of probability densities. NKEs learn general Lévy-type stochastic forcing directly through the operator structure of the KFE, and enable parallel-in-time training via a Lagrangian Galerkin projection and operator splitting. We evaluate NKEs on several stochastic benchmarks, including systems with coupled noise and jump processes, and verify that NKEs provide flexible models that accurately recover deterministic and stochastic dynamics with competitive predictive accuracy and improved training efficiency. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [225] [Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization for Offline RL Under Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19199)
*Li-Rong Zhou,Qin-Wen Luo,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于不确定性估计的保守查询与自适应正则化框架（CQAR-UE），用于改进离线强化学习中的动作偏好查询与策略优化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是离线强化学习（offline RL）中的动作偏好查询与策略优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离线强化学习中的动作偏好查询与策略优化；应用场景：D4RL基准测试中的连续控制任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Morse network（用于不确定性估计）；与CQL（Conservative Q-Learning）集成。

Lightweight method: 摘要中自称"a lightweight framework"，但这里的"lightweight"指的是框架设计上的轻量（减少额外计算开销），并非模型压缩领域的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与遥感在轨部署所需的轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：D4RL benchmark（离线强化学习标准基准）；评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标，但提到"superior or competitive performance across a wide range of tasks"。

Experiments: 与CQL集成，在D4RL benchmark上进行了广泛评估，在多种任务上取得优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明方法的计算开销、实时性等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然论文中使用了"lightweight"一词，但此处的轻量概念与遥感在轨部署中的模型轻量化（减少参数量、FLOPs、延迟以适应星上资源约束）完全不同。该论文属于离线强化学习领域，与遥感目标检测任务无关。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn an effective policy from a static dataset, but its performance is fundamentally limited by dataset coverage. Action preference queries leverage expert feedback without additional environment interaction, enabling policy improvement during offline training. However, existing methods still face two key challenges: selecting informative preference queries and effectively exploiting the collected feedback. Current approaches typically rely only on the distance between policy actions and dataset actions for query selection, while enforcing fixed constraints that keep the policy close to queried preferences. Such strategies often lead to unstable policy updates and integrate poorly with value regularization. To address these limitations, we propose Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization under Uncertainty Estimation, a lightweight framework that jointly improves preference querying and preference exploitation. Specifically, we employ a Morse network to estimate the uncertainty of policy actions with respect to the offline dataset. Based on this uncertainty, we introduce a conservative query strategy that selectively queries actions near the dataset to preserve Bellman-update stability, together with an uncertainty-aware adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically adjusts data-level constraints during policy optimization. We integrate our framework with CQL and evaluate it extensively on the D4RL benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate superior or competitive performance across a wide range of tasks.

</details>


### [226] [AdaFlash: Adaptive Speculative Decoding via On-Policy Distilled Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19223)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Chen Chen,Jiacheng Sun,Zhenhua Dong,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaFlash框架，通过on-policy蒸馏和自适应长度头解决扩散式投机解码中双向注意力导致的高方差问题，加速大语言模型推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）推理加速中的投机解码（speculative decoding），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及星上部署或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型推理加速（speculative decoding）；应用场景：LLM文本生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 扩散式草稿模型（diffusion drafter）+ 目标大语言模型（target LLM），采用双向注意力机制（bidirectional attention），自适应长度头（adaptive length head）。

Lightweight method: 投机解码（speculative decoding）范式，扩散式草稿模型（diffusion drafter）单次前向生成草稿序列，on-policy蒸馏（OPD）算法，自适应长度头动态调整候选序列长度。但这些方法面向LLM文本生成加速，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无直接关系。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及吞吐量（throughput）提升约66%，以及高并发场景下的加速比（speedup rate），但这些指标面向LLM服务端推理，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：接受率（acceptance rate）、加速比（speedup rate）、吞吐量（throughput）。

Experiments: 实验表明AdaFlash在部署中持续提升加速比，高并发场景下吞吐量比之前SOTA方法提升约66%。但摘要未给出具体实验设置、对比方法列表、消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 方法针对LLM文本生成场景设计，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务；3. 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标；4. 未涉及任何遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标（如mAP）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：1）on-policy蒸馏（OPD）中利用reverse-KL divergence稳定收敛的思想，在遥感目标检测模型的知识蒸馏中可能有一定参考价值；2）自适应长度头动态调整候选长度的思路，在遥感在轨检测中可类比为根据场景复杂度动态调整推理路径或模型深度。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向差距较大。

Abstract: Speculative decoding, in which a lightweight draft model first generates a draft sequence that is then verified in parallel by the target model, has become a prevalent paradigm for accelerating large language model inference. Recent work such as DFlash further boosts drafting efficiency by leveraging diffusion drafters, whose parallel denoising mechanism enables draft generation in a single forward pass. In this work, we uncover a central pitfall of diffusion drafters: bidirectional attention is a double-edged sword. On one hand, it endows the model with parallel generation and global contextual modeling capabilities; on the other hand, this inherent global dependency introduces high variance at both the domain-level and the token-level: acceptance rates fluctuate substantially across different domains, and draft token quality also varies heterogeneously at different token positions. To tackle this issue, we propose AdaFlash framework, comprising two components: (i) an on-policy distillation (OPD) algorithm with reverse-KL divergence tailored for diffusion drafters, bringing stable convergence and effectively reducing domain-level variance; and (ii) an adaptive length head that dynamically adjusts the candidate sequence length on the fly, substantially lowering the verification cost of the target model and handling token-level variance. Experiments demonstrate that AdaFlash consistently improves speedup rate during deployment, with especially significant gains in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving up to approximately 66% higher throughput than previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [227] [S3: Stable Subgoal Selection by Constraining Uncertainty of Coarse Dynamics in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19232)
*Kshitij Kumar Srivastava,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于粗粒度动力学感知内在奖励的分层强化学习方法，用于改进高层智能体的子目标选择策略。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于强化学习/分层强化学习方法论研究，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文研究的是分层强化学习中的子目标选择问题，应用场景为长时域复杂任务（long-horizon complex tasks），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等具体任务。

Model architecture: 分层强化学习（HRL）框架，包含高层智能体（high-level agent）和低层智能体（low-level agent）。高层使用混合密度网络（Mixture Density Network, MDN）建模粗粒度动力学的不确定性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明，所提出的动力学感知内在奖励方法在非平稳长时域环境中优于现有SOTA分层强化学习方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。论文聚焦于强化学习中的探索与信用分配问题，不涉及视觉感知或模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) intends to separate strategic planning from primitive execution. It has been widely successful in solving long-horizon and complex tasks, where flat-RL algorithms have difficulty in learning. However, while the low-level agent in HRL benefits from dense feedback and abundant trial opportunities, the high-level agent receives sparse, delayed feedback from the environment and its performance depends on the low-level execution capability. In this paper, we study whether subgoal selection by the high-level agent can be performed more strategically, by providing it with dynamics-aware intrinsic motivation. Since motivation based on primitive transition dynamics would require broad coverage of the state-action space, we propose to use coarse dynamics, i.e., environment transitions aggregated over multiple steps at the temporal scale at which the high-level agent operates. This approach stabilizes the high-level policy by learning to minimize the predictive uncertainty associated with the coarse dynamics, and provides a guided structure for navigation. We model the predictive uncertainty by evaluating different dispersion metrics as approximated by a Mixture Density Network (MDN). Empirically, we observe that a dense, dynamics-aware intrinsic reward leads to risk-averse subgoal selection, enabling it to outperform state-of-the-art HRL methods in non-stationary long-horizon environments.

</details>


### [228] [In-Context Time Series Classification with Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19234)
*Joscha Cüppers,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MASHT流水线，将随机卷积变换（MultiRocket/Hydra）与预训练表格基础模型结合，用于时间序列分类，无需任务特定训练即可达到SOTA性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于通用时间序列分类（医疗信号、工业监控、传感器活动识别），与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景无关；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列分类（Time Series Classification）。应用场景：医疗信号分析、工业监控、基于传感器的活动识别。传感器类型：通用时间序列传感器（非遥感卫星传感器）。目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆/小目标/旋转框检测）完全无关。

Model architecture: MASHT流水线：MultiRocket + Hydra 随机卷积特征提取 → 预训练表格基础模型（tabular foundation model）进行上下文推理。核心创新在于用预训练基础模型替代传统线性分类器来利用随机卷积特征。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。相反，它依赖预训练的大规模表格基础模型进行推理。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。由于依赖预训练表格基础模型，推理开销可能较大，不适合星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未说明具体数据集名称（仅提及univariate和multivariate时间序列数据集）。评价指标：平均排名（average rank），与HIVE-COTE 2.0等基线对比。

Experiments: 在单变量时间序列分类任务上，MASHT达到SOTA水平，平均排名低于HIVE-COTE 2.0。在多变量数据集上，与最强参考方法保持高度竞争力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 依赖预训练基础模型，推理成本可能较高；3. 仅在时间序列分类任务上验证，未涉及目标检测或遥感场景理解；4. 未提供任何部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：随机卷积变换（如MultiRocket）作为一种轻量级特征提取思路，或许可以探索用于遥感图像的特征表示，但论文本身并未朝这个方向做任何工作。整体而言，不建议在本课题中参考此论文。

Abstract: Time series classification is central to domains like medical signal analysis, industrial monitoring, and sensor-based activity recognition, where class information manifests as localized shapes, specific frequencies, temporal shifts, or complex cross-channel interactions. Random convolutional transforms efficiently map these sequences to fixed-dimensional tabular features but are traditionally paired with simple linear classifiers. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model can more effectively harness these rich representations.
  We propose MASHT, a pipeline that marries MultiRocket and Hydra features with the power of in-context tabular foundation models. By leveraging a pretrained tabular foundation model, our approach completely bypasses task-specific model training, requiring only feature extraction and direct inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASHT matches state-of-the-art time series classification baselines on univariate tasks, achieving a lower average rank than HIVE-COTE 2.0. On multivariate datasets, MASHT remains highly competitive with the strongest reference methods.

</details>


### [229] [DBMol: Design of High-Affinity, Target-Specific Small Molecules through Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19237)
*Yiming Qin,Kai Yi,Miruna Cretu,Sjors H. W. Scheres,Pietro Liò,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DBMol框架，利用AlphaFold-3/Boltz-2等结构预测模型作为优化信号，通过交替优化与投影生成高亲和力小分子配体。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于药物发现中的小分子配体设计（de novo molecular design），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：小分子配体从头设计（de novo drug design）；场景：药物发现/计算化学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：小分子配体与蛋白质口袋的结合。

Model architecture: DBMol框架包含两个阶段：(1) 优化阶段——基于结构预测模型（Boltz-2）的梯度优化，改进口袋特异性相互作用和预测结合亲和力；(2) 投影阶段——流匹配模型（flow-matching model）将优化后的分子图映射为离散且化学有效的小分子。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：预测结合亲和力（Boltz-2 affinity proxy）、口袋覆盖率（pocket coverage）、分子多样性（molecular diversity）、held-out metrics（包括AF3-based evaluation）。

Experiments: 实验表明DBMol能有效优化Boltz-2亲和力代理，生成具有强预测亲和力和特异性的分子；相比无条件生成，显著提升口袋覆盖率并保持分子多样性；在held-out指标上具有竞争力。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显的问题是：该方法完全面向计算化学/药物设计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何交集。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于计算化学/药物设计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、数据、方法、评价体系上均无交叉。

Abstract: Designing small molecule ligands that bind with high affinity to specific protein pockets is a fundamental goal in drug discovery, as small molecules constitute a major fraction of approved therapeutics. Recent breakthroughs in structure prediction, such as AlphaFold-3 and Boltz-2, enable accurate biomolecular interaction prediction and show promise as foundation models for downstream tasks, including binding affinity prediction. We propose to leverage these models and introduce DBMol, a new structure predictor-guided framework for de novo small molecule design. DBMol formulates an alternating optimization and projection process. In the optimization stage, DBMol starts from an initial molecule and uses gradient-based optimization to improve pocket-specific interactions and predicted binding affinity using a structure prediction model. In the projection stage, a flow-matching model maps the optimized molecular graph to discrete and chemically valid molecules. Experiments show that DBMol effectively optimizes the Boltz-2 affinity proxy and generates molecules with strong predicted affinity and specificity under Boltz-2 evaluation. To reduce self-confirmation bias, we further evaluate generated molecules using held-out metrics, including AF3-based evaluation. DBMol substantially improves pocket coverage while maintaining molecular diversity over unconditional generation, and is competitive under held-out metrics despite the absence of reference-ligand supervision. These results support the promise of structure prediction models as effective optimization signals for de novo molecular design.

</details>


### [230] [Thermodynamics-Informed Input Reparameterization for Neural Prediction of Real-Fluid Thermodynamic Properties in Supercritical Combustion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19241)
*Haoze Zhang,Han Li,Ke Xiao,Yangchen Xu,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种热力学知识引导的输入重参数化策略（TAIR），用理想气体近似替代原始焓坐标，提升超临界燃烧模拟中神经网络代理的预测精度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究超临界燃烧模拟中的热力学属性预测，属于计算流体力学/燃烧仿真领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。虽然使用了神经网络代理（surrogate）来替代传统计算，但应用场景、输入输出形式、优化目标都与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：超临界燃烧模拟中的热力学属性（温度T、密度ρ、压缩系数ψ）预测；场景：甲烷-氧反流火焰仿真；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 神经网络代理（Neural-network surrogates），具体架构摘要未说明。核心创新是输入重参数化策略TAIR，将原始焓坐标替换为目标匹配的热力学坐标（温度网络用定比热理想气体混合焓反推的温度估计，密度和压缩系数网络用理想气体密度估计）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。虽然使用了神经网络代理替代传统数值计算可视为一种计算加速，但并非面向模型轻量化的方法。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：超临界甲烷-氧反流火焰仿真数据；评价指标：held-out RMSE（均方根误差）；实验设置：对比raw-input基线、target-inconsistent cross-reparameterization控制实验。

Experiments: TAIR方法在T、ρ、ψ上的held-out RMSE分别降低约1.5倍、2.0倍、7.5倍；在未见过的应变率火焰上，对应降低3.6倍、14.5倍、6.0倍。目标不一致的控制实验表现更差，说明增益来自热力学匹配的输入设计而非通用预处理。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。从方法本身看，TAIR依赖于特定物理领域知识（理想气体近似），通用性有限。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然领域不同，但"用物理先验知识指导输入重参数化"的思想在遥感轻量化中可能有间接启发：例如在遥感目标检测中，可以利用遥感图像的物理特性（如地物光谱先验、几何先验）对输入进行变换，帮助轻量网络更高效地学习。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Real-fluid thermodynamic property evaluation is a major computational cost in supercritical combustion simulations. In the enthalpy-based pressure-correction formulation, the closure evaluates temperature T, density $ρ$, and compressibility coefficient $ψ$ from the solver state (h,p,Y) through enthalpy-temperature inversion and repeated real-fluid equation-of-state evaluations. Neural-network surrogates offer fixed-cost inference, but direct mapping from (h,p,Y) to $(T,ρ,ψ)$ must capture the enthalpy-temperature relation and non-ideal equation-of-state response, resulting in a complex regression problem. This work introduces a thermodynamics-informed input reparameterization strategy, termed target-aligned input reparameterization (TAIR). TAIR replaces the raw enthalpy coordinate of each property network with a target-matched thermodynamic coordinate: the temperature network uses a temperature estimate obtained by inverting a constant-$c_p$ ideal-gas mixture enthalpy approximation, whereas the density and compressibility networks use an ideal-gas density estimate. These algebraic transformations use only solver-available variables and species constants, guiding the networks to learn real-fluid departures from ideal-gas baselines rather than reconstructing the full closure from raw enthalpy. The method is assessed using supercritical methane-oxygen counterflow flame data against a raw-input baseline and target-inconsistent cross-reparameterization controls. TAIR reduces held-out RMSE by factors of about 1.5, 2.0, and 7.5 for T, $ρ$, and $ψ$, respectively. For an unseen strain-rate flame within the augmented thermodynamic envelope, the corresponding factors are 3.6, 14.5, and 6.0. The target-inconsistent controls perform worse, indicating that the gains arise from thermodynamically matched input design rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [231] [Toward Auditable Fraud Detection: Combining Graph Features, Model Explanations, and Agentic Case Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19266)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究基于PaySim数据集的欺诈检测分层流水线，结合梯度提升分类器、图结构特征、自编码器异常信号、TreeSHAP解释和LLM调查代理，发现各组件仅在特定条件下有效，且LLM代理的合理推理并不等同于更优决策。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是金融欺诈检测（fraud detection），使用PaySim金融交易数据集，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：欺诈检测（fraud detection）；应用场景：金融交易；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：欺诈交易/正常交易。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 分层流水线（layered pipeline）：梯度提升分类器（gradient-boosted classifier）+ 图结构特征（graph-derived structural features）+ 自编码器异常信号（autoencoder-based anomaly signal）+ TreeSHAP解释 + 有界LLM调查代理（bounded LLM investigation agent）。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PaySim（金融交易模拟数据集）；评价指标：Average Precision（平均精度）、准确率（accuracy）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 主要发现：去除模拟器特定平衡捷径后，图特征和异常信号在完整测试集上未提升AP；但在中间分数子集上改善了欺诈排序；注入多账户欺诈环实验中，图结构特征恢复了所有注入交易，而基线漏掉了约1/4；LLM调查代理准确率65.0%低于直接阈值分类器的71.7%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关结论或方法。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是：论文中"去除数据捷径"的思路（identify and remove a simulator-specific balance shortcut）在遥感数据集构建中也有类似问题（如数据分布偏差），但与本课题核心方向关联极弱。

Abstract: Fraud detection systems must scale with rising transaction volume while remaining explainable and reviewable. We study a layered pipeline on the PaySim dataset that combines a gradient-boosted classifier, graph-derived structural features, an autoencoder-based anomaly signal, TreeSHAP explanations, and a bounded LLM investigation agent applied to cases the classifier scores uncertainly. Before any model comparison, we identify and remove a simulator-specific balance shortcut that would otherwise inflate baseline performance. After this correction, neither the graph features nor the anomaly signal improves Average Precision on the full test set. Both, however, rank fraud better within the subset of cases receiving intermediate baseline scores. In a controlled experiment with injected multi-account fraud rings, engineered structural features recover all injected test transactions, while the tabular baseline misses roughly a quarter of them. The investigation agent underperforms direct thresholding of the classifier it relies on, reaching 65.0% accuracy against 71.7% on a balanced 60-case sample, despite having access to model explanations, graph context, and retrieved reference cases. Of the eight decisions the agent changed, six replaced correct classifier outputs with errors, and it produced a coherent written rationale in each case. An exploratory disagreement-based escalation rule flagged two of these agent errors for human review without flagging any correct decision. We conclude that each component of a layered fraud system contributes only under specific conditions, and that a plausible rationale from an investigation agent is not evidence of a better decision.

</details>


### [232] [Riemannian Deep Learning:Modules, Networks, and Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19305)
*Chen Ziheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个黎曼深度学习的统一框架，涵盖可重用神经模块、流形特定网络架构和底层几何设计，并在视觉、信号处理、图学习和基因组学中验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于黎曼流形上的深度学习理论框架，虽然提及视觉应用，但完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、星上部署等任何与在轨推理相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感或目标检测任务。应用场景为视觉、信号处理、图学习、基因组学，与遥感卫星无关。传感器类型和目标类别均未提及。

Model architecture: 提出黎曼深度学习统一框架，包括：可重用神经模块（批归一化推广到Lie群和gyrogroups、多项逻辑回归推广到SPD流形和一般黎曼流形）、流形特定网络架构（双曲空间的无约束模型、Busemann双曲学习、满秩相关矩阵网络）、底层几何设计（SPD流形上的可学习Log-Euclidean几何和基于Cholesky的快速稳定几何）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。虽然提到"computationally efficient"和"fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries"，但这是相对于传统黎曼几何计算而言，并非面向边缘/星上部署的轻量化设计。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及通过数值实验和在视觉、信号处理、图学习、基因组学中的应用进行验证，未给出具体实验结果和对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文是纯理论性的黎曼深度学习框架研究，缺乏对遥感场景、目标检测任务和模型轻量化的任何讨论。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。如果未来研究方向涉及流形学习（如双曲空间用于遥感场景理解、SPD流形用于SAR图像协方差特征），该论文提供的理论框架可能有间接参考价值。但就"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"这一具体课题而言，几乎没有可借鉴点。

Abstract: Deep neural networks on manifold-valued representations have attracted growing interest, but many basic components remain tied to specific manifolds, rely on Euclidean approximations, or require costly and numerically fragile geometric operations. This thesis develops a unified framework for Riemannian deep learning from three complementary perspectives: reusable neural modules, manifold-specific network architectures, and the design of underlying geometries. It generalizes batch normalization from Euclidean spaces and individual manifolds to broad classes of Lie groups and gyrogroups, and extends multinomial logistic regression from Euclidean space to SPD manifolds and then to general Riemannian manifolds. It further develops neural networks for several important geometric representations, including an unconstrained model of hyperbolic space, Busemann-based hyperbolic learning, and full-rank correlation matrices. Finally, it introduces adaptive and computationally efficient Riemannian metrics on SPD manifolds, including learnable Log-Euclidean geometries and fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries. The proposed methods are supported by theoretical analysis and validated through numerical experiments and applications in vision, signal processing, graph learning, and genomics.

</details>


### [233] [ISO: An RLVR-Native Optimization Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19331)
*Hanqing Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Zhizhou Sha,Sagnik Mukherjee,Xinyuan Song,David González-Martínez,Xiaoxia Wu,Yuandong Tian,Shiwei Liu,David Z. Pan,Zhangyang "Atlas" Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等谱优化(ISO)框架，研究强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)中权重谱的继承机制，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化均无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的强化学习优化方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型强化学习优化（RLVR）；应用场景：reasoning and coding tasks；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的大语言模型（1.5B-8B参数规模），关注权重矩阵的奇异值分解(SVD)结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是RLVR训练优化方法，非模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为aggregate accuracy。

Experiments: 在Qwen3-8B-Base上，AdamW 270步达0.495准确率，ISO-AdamW 100步达相同准确率，210步达0.509。实验规模1.5B-8B参数。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来在轨模型需要在线自适应学习（如on-orbit fine-tuning），其优化策略可参考谱继承思想，但与本课题核心方向距离过远。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is rapidly advancing the reasoning capabilities of language models, yet the optimization layer that converts reward feedback into weight-space updates remains poorly understood. Building on our prior analysis (Zhu et al., 2025), we study this missing layer through the singular structure of model weights and identify spectral inheritance: RLVR can reuse the base model's weight spectra while acquiring new behavior through changes in the associated input and output singular frames.
  We operationalize spectral inheritance as Isospectral Optimization (ISO), an RLVR-native, fixed-spectrum optimization framework with complementary offline and online instantiations. Offline, ISO-Merger combines the frame changes of shared-base specialists into a single fixed-spectrum model, requiring no post-merge data, rollouts, gradient updates, or on-policy distillation (OPD). It recovers complementary specialist capabilities and achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the compared data-free merging methods. Online, ISO-Optimizer applies a chosen base optimizer, including AdamW and Muon, to the frame variables while keeping the base spectra fixed. Across reasoning and coding tasks ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, ISO-Optimizer improves accuracy in the reported runs and reaches matched scores with substantially fewer training steps. On Qwen3-8B-Base, AdamW reaches an aggregate accuracy of 0.495 after 270 training steps. ISO-AdamW reaches the same accuracy after only 100 training steps and improves further to 0.509 after 210 training steps. Together, ISO offers a concrete answer to RLVR's missing optimization layer: rather than inheriting pre-training optimization wholesale, design post-training around the structure of reward-driven adaptation: inherit the spectrum, optimize the frames.

</details>

### [234] [Blind Adaptive Equalization in Additive Impulsive Noise Using the Logarithmic Product Fractional-Moment (LP-FM) Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18478)
*Shafayat Abrar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对数乘积分数阶矩统计(LP-FMS)准则的盲均衡算法(NBEA-SAS)，用于抑制对称α稳定分布脉冲噪声下的码间干扰。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是通信信号处理中的盲均衡算法，不涉及遥感、卫星图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The blind mitigation of inter-symbol interference in additive white impulsive noise modeled by a symmetric $α$-stable (S$α$S) distribution is investigated. A novel logarithmic product fractional-moment statistics (LP-FMS) criterion is proposed by combining complementary fractional-moment statistics with logarithmic normalization in a constrained optimization framework. Based on this criterion, a normalized blind equalization algorithm for symmetric alpha-stable noise (NBEA-SAS) is derived using stochastic gradient ascent with recursive fractional-moment estimation and Bussgang-consistent constrained adaptation. Simulation results for $64$-APSK signaling over fractionally spaced multipath microwave channels show that the proposed algorithm converges faster than FLOS-CMA, RAW-CMA, and NBEA-GG while achieving a comparable steady-state residual intersymbol interference floor under both moderately and highly impulsive S$α$S noise conditions. The results demonstrate that the proposed LP-FMS criterion provides a robust framework for blind adaptive equalization in impulsive noise environments.

</details>

Abstract: We address optimal link scheduling in large-scale wireless networks. The goal is to schedule transmissions over a time horizon so that to maximize sum rate while ensuring that average rates of each customer attain a minimum rate requirement. To this end, we formulate a constrained optimization problem and solve it using Lagrangian duality. Common primal-dual approaches lead to time invariant policies. Our constraint requires all links transmit a fraction of the time while avoiding interference, which calls for time-varying policies across time slots. We propose an iterative algorithm to sample optimal sequences of schedules and dual variables. The scheduling decisions are parameterized using a Graph Neural Network. We incorporate state-augmentation techniques to learn said parameterization, introducing dual variables as dynamic inputs to the policy. This augmentation enables the GNN to adapt scheduling decisions over time, balancing constraint satisfaction with performance maximization. We validate our approach through extensive numerical simulations, benchmarking against several baselines and considering varying constraint levels.

</details>

Abstract: Data-aided channel estimation enhances spectral efficiency by reusing detected symbols as virtual pilots. In this process, selecting only reliable symbols is crucial to prevent misdetected symbols from corrupting the channel estimate. However, conventional methods rely exclusively on physical-layer statistics. Beyond physical-layer information, transmitted payloads possess inherent semantic structures that can be exploited to resolve detection errors. In this paper, we propose a novel semantic-aware channel estimation framework for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems that utilizes a fine-tuned large language model (LLM) to perform reliable symbol selection and correction based on semantic information. The framework employs a two-layer mechanism: one layer selects reliable decoded symbols through semantic verification, while the other selects accurately LLM-corrected symbols by cross-validating them against the received signal using physical-layer information. We prove that corrected symbols yield a strictly larger expected reduction in estimation error than initially correctly decoded symbols. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly outperforms conventional data-aided schemes in both normalized mean squared error and bit error rate, closely approaching the performance of an oracle estimator.

</details>


### [237] [Joint Synchronization and Sensing in Networked ISAC via Structured Canonical Polyadic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18680)
*Lin Chen,Yifan Liang,Hongbin Li*

Abstract: Networked integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) offers significant potential for next-generation wireless systems. By exploiting spatial diversity through the cooperation of multiple base stations (BSs), this architecture expands coverage and achieves enhanced sensing performance. However, accurate sensing in networked ISAC requires time-frequency synchronization among BSs. Existing synchronization methods for networked ISAC suffer from inter-path interference caused by sensing channel compression. To address this problem, this paper proposes a structured canonical polyadic decomposition (SCPD) algorithm that effectively separates the multipath components of the sensing channel. Benefiting from this separation, SCPD achieves joint network-level synchronization and multi-target parameter estimation. We establish theoretical identifiability conditions for SCPD and show that it asymptotically achieves the Cramér-Rao bound. Furthermore, by incorporating parameters estimated from different BS pairs, we propose a multi-target tracking algorithm designed for the continuous operation of the system. The proposed algorithm tracks both the trajectories and velocities of moving targets by leveraging geometric diversity. Utilizing tracking results from the previous snapshot, an adaptive beamforming scheme is also developed to improve tracking performance in the next snapshot. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior accuracy and outlier robustness for both synchronization and sensing in networked ISAC, outperforming traditional approaches.

</details>


### [238] [Cyclic-Prefix-Free OFDM With Tail-Reuse Reconstruction for Distributed Acoustic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18687)
*Ziang Chen,Zhiyang Xue,Zhongxing Tian,Zeyu Feng,Dongdong Zou,Yi Cai,Huan Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM分布式声学传感（DAS）中去除循环前缀（CP）的波形设计，通过尾重用实现信道重构，提升无混叠振动频率上限，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于光纤传感（distributed acoustic sensing, DAS）和OFDM通信信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任何视觉检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体深度学习模型架构。文中涉及OFDM波形设计、尾重用（tail reuse）信道重构方法，属于信号处理算法，非神经网络模型。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：5.2 km数值仿真配置 + 5.2 km相干DAS实验链路；评价指标：无混叠振动频率上限（Nyquist limit）、重构SNR、差分相位可靠性、振动事件定位精度（5063.7 m和5070.1 m处PZT振动定位）。

Experiments: 数值仿真验证了尾重用方法、折叠边界预测、100个振动事件（800 Hz-8800 Hz）的恢复；带宽缩放仿真表明联合精细空间观测可改善差分相位可靠性和重构SNR；实验在5.2 km DAS链路上以111.984 MHz OFDM带宽盲定位500 Hz和3 kHz PZT振动，恢复波形和频谱。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。摘要本身未讨论遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化的任何内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于光纤传感与OFDM通信信号处理领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) enables frequency-domain reconstruction of the distributed Rayleigh backscatter channel in coherent distributed acoustic sensing (DAS), but an explicitly transmitted cyclic prefix (CP) lengthens the probing period and reduces the slow-time Nyquist limit of each range cell. We investigate a repeated cyclic-prefix-free OFDM waveform for DAS, in which the tail of the preceding useful block serves as a virtual cyclic extension. A finite-memory range condition for tail-reuse reconstruction is derived, and circular folding is identified when the useful period is shorter than the channel memory. For fixed useful-block length and fiber memory, removing the explicit CP increases the period-limited highest unaliased vibration frequency without changing the occupied-bandwidth-limited spatial resolution. In a 5.2-km numerical configuration, a 75-MSa/s processing rate and a 4096-sample useful block give a 54.61-us probing period and a 9.16-kHz slow-time Nyquist limit. Simulations verify tail reuse, the predicted folding boundary, and recovery of 100 vibration events from 800 Hz to 8800 Hz. Bandwidth-scaling simulations further show that joint processing of fine spatial observations improves differential-phase reliability and reconstruction SNR at a fixed reporting interval. Experiments on a 5.2-km coherent DAS link with 111.984-MHz occupied OFDM bandwidth blindly localize 500-Hz and 3-kHz PZT-induced vibrations at 5063.7 m and 5070.1 m, respectively, and recover their waveforms and spectra. The results demonstrate feasible tail-reuse channel reconstruction and quantify the extension of the unaliased vibration bandwidth.

</details>


### [239] [Non-Square UPA-Enabled XL-MIMO Systems: Anisotropic Near-Field Characterization, Fundamental Limits, and Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18736)
*Yilong Liu,Xi Yang,Jing Xu,Jun Zhang,Shi jin*

Model architecture: 研究的是非方形UPA天线阵列的几何结构及其对各向异性近场特性的影响，不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构。提出了3D各向异性近场码本用于信道估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。文中提到的"低复杂度"是指所设计的3D各向异性近场码本相比3D极域码本降低了计算复杂度，但这属于通信信号处理层面的复杂度优化，与深度学习模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等）。该研究面向地面通信基站，非星上场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用任何标准数据集。评价指标包括：有效波束聚焦距离、渐近有效自由度、Cramer-Rao下界（距离估计）、3D位置误差界（PEB）、信道估计精度与复杂度对比。

Experiments: 通过数值仿真验证了所提出的3D各向异性近场码本在信道估计中能达到与3D极域码本相当的精度，同时复杂度更低。摘要未提供具体的数值结果对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。从通信角度看，摘要未说明实际硬件实现中的功耗、实时性等工程约束。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的点是：论文中"非方形阵列导致各向异性"的思想或许可以类比思考非对称卷积核或非方形特征图在轻量检测网络中的设计，但关联非常牵强，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) is crucial for next-generation communication systems. In practice, the deployment of non-square uniform planar arrays (UPAs) fundamentally alters wavefront characteristics and induces anisotropic beamfocusing capability along different axes due to the aperture disparity originating from the non-square array geometry. To fully uncover the performance impact of such non-square geometry and thus unleash the potential of the non-square UPAs, we investigate the anisotropic near-field characteristics, fundamental limits, and channel estimation for non-square UPA-enabled XL-MIMO systems. First, we derive the effective beamfocusing distances for the long and short axes of the array. Interestingly, the radiation space of a non-square UPA can be partitioned into three regions, i.e., the fully near-field, the anisotropic near-field, and the far-field regions, and the anisotropic region asymptotically dominates the overall near-field space as the array aspect ratio increases. Then, the asymptotic effective degree of freedom for non-square UPA-enabled XL-MIMO systems is provided, which reveals that distance-domain multiplexing is governed by the long-axis aperture in the large array aspect ratio regime. Furthermore, the closed-form Cramer-Rao bound for distance estimation and the three-dimensional (3D) position error bound (PEB) are derived to reveal the geometry-induced performance trade-offs among distance, azimuth, and elevation estimation, based on which the optimal array aspect ratio that minimizes the 3D PEB is determined. Finally, by exploiting the anisotropic wavefront properties, we design a 3D anisotropic near-field codebook to facilitate low-complexity channel estimation for non-square UPAs. Numerical results validate that the proposed codebook achieves comparable accuracy to the 3D polar-domain codebook at reduced complexity.

</details>

Abstract: Environment division multiple access (EDMA) has emerged as a promising multiple access paradigm, which mitigates inter-user interference by dynamically adjusting pinching antenna (PA) positions to the underlying propagation environment. This paper investigates a multi-user PA-enabled EDMA framework that accounts for probabilistic line-of-sight (LoS) blockages, random non-LoS (NLoS) scattering, and practical inwaveguide attenuation. With the aim of maximizing the total information rate, we formulate a joint PA deployment and power allocation problem subject to statistical rate outage constraints, the transmit power budget, and the feasible deployment region of PAs. We first consider a canonical two-user two-PA scenario and derive closed-form expressions for the outage probabilities, followed by a low-complexity projected gradient descent (PGD)-based algorithm to address the reformulated problem. Then, we extend our design to the general multi-user multi-PA scenario and derive tractable approximations for the outage probabilities by assuming interfering links to be NLoS and applying the Chernoff bounding technique, where a successive convex approximation (SCA)-based algorithm is proposed to handle the resulting nonconvex problem. Simulations validate the superiority of the proposed PA-enabled EDMA design and the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms. Specifically, both the PGD-based algorithm and the SCA-based algorithm achieve near-optimal performance in comparison with the exhaustive search. Furthermore, the PA-enabled EDMA design yields significant performance gains over both PA-enabled and conventional time division multiple access designs.

</details>

Abstract: Low-complexity channel state information acquisition is crucial for extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) systems. However, practical deployments of non-square uniform planar arrays (UPAs) in hybrid-field environments face prohibitive computational complexity and degraded estimation accuracy due to limited elevation angle-of-arrival (AoA) resolution and deteriorated channel sparsity. To tackle these challenges, we propose a low-complexity channel estimation framework. First, an antenna-domain extrapolation scheme synthesizes a virtually enlarged vertical aperture via the spatial correlation among adjacent elements, breaking the elevation resolution limit. The framework then disentangles the parameter coupling by transforming the two-dimensional joint search into two sequential one-dimensional searches. Specifically, elevation AoAs are extracted via an extrapolation-enhanced discrete Fourier transform-Newtonized orthogonal matching pursuit (NOMP) algorithm along the virtually enlarged vertical uniform linear array (ULA), while azimuth AoAs, ranges, and gains are acquired utilizing a discrete fractional Fourier transform-NOMP algorithm along a horizontal ULA. A subspace fitting-driven path matching algorithm pairs these decoupled parameters. To overcome the accuracy bottleneck of the antenna-domain scheme, a correlation-domain extrapolation scheme is further developed by exploiting the structural properties of the spatial correlation matrix to decouple the near-field quadratic and azimuth phase components, yielding a noise-suppressed virtual array. Numerical results validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [242] [Low-Altitude UAV-Assisted Bistatic ISAC: Closed-form 3D CRLB and Coverage Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18766)
*Haoyu Jiang,Hengyou Kong,Xiaoli Xu,Yong Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

Abstract: This paper investigates the fundamental performance limits of three-dimensional (3D) localization in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Specifically, a base station (BS) estimates the 3D position of a sensing target with the aid of a UAV acting as a flexible aerial anchor node. We derive a closed-form expression for the 3D Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB), which explicitly quantifies the achievable localization accuracy as a function of both the UAV's location and the target's position. The CRLB is shown to decompose naturally into three distinct components, arising from signal propagation delay, angular measurements, and their coupling effect, respectively. To validate the analytical results, we consider a representative orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM)-based ISAC system and demonstrate that the derived CRLB closely predicts the performance of maximum-likelihood estimation across diverse geometric configurations and UAV mobility patterns. Furthermore, we introduce the notion of CRLB-constrained sensing coverage to characterize the spatial region within which a prescribed localization accuracy can be guaranteed. Through local boundary approximations and coverage-size evaluations, we reveal how UAV displacement, altitude, and the CRLB threshold jointly shape the extent and geometry of the reliable sensing region.

</details>


### [243] [Data-aided Channel Estimation and Sensing With Sparse Bayesian Learning for AFDM-ISAC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18881)
*Yirui Luo,Yong Liang Guan,Yao Ge,Yonghong Jiang,Lingsheng Meng,David González G*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种数据辅助的网格进化稀疏贝叶斯学习（D-GESBL）方案，用于AFDM波形下双弥散信道中的信道估计与感知参数联合获取。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising waveform for next-generation integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. However, it becomes challenging to improve spectral efficiency while simultaneously obtaining accurate channel and sensing-related parameters, particularly in doubly-dispersive channels with fractional delays and fractional Doppler shifts. To tackle this challenge, by formulating the channel estimation task as a multiple measurement vector (MMV) off-grid sparse recovery problem, we propose a data-aided grid-evolution sparse Bayesian learning (D-GESBL) scheme for channel estimation and sensing under a superimposed pilot framework. Specifically, we develop an efficient data-aided iterative receiver, in which reliably decoded data symbols are fed back as additional pseudo-pilot information to assist channel estimation and sensing. To mitigate off-grid mismatch and improve the overall estimation accuracy, we develop a grid evolution procedure that iteratively adjusts the virtual grids in the discrete affine Fourier (DAF) domain according to the estimated off-grid components. Furthermore, by integrating the generalized approximate message passing (GAMP) algorithm into the proposed SBL framework, we also develop a low-complexity data-aided GAMP-based grid-evolution SBL (D-GAMP-GESBL) algorithm. Finally, the numerical results validate the effectiveness of our proposed schemes and demonstrate their superiority over existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [244] [Convex Optimization-Based Procedures for Non-Convex Quadratic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18933)
*Mahmoud Zaher,Emil Björnson*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。其核心是数学优化理论（QCQP）在信号处理和无线通信中的应用，属于纯数学/通信领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Abstract: Mathematical optimization plays a fundamental role in signal processing and wireless communications, serving as an essential framework for the systematic design of modern systems. Many design challenges in these fields, as well as in many others, can naturally be formulated as optimization problems. Over the years, the advancements in signal processing applications have significantly changed the structure and complexity of these optimization problems, creating new challenges in their analysis, understanding, and solution \cite{liu2024survey}. Consequently, the rapid development of sophisticated optimization theories and algorithms tailored to the demands of next-generation systems is crucial.
  Quadratic optimization problems constitute one of the most important classes of optimization problems in modern engineering systems. In signal processing and communications, quadratic forms naturally emerge when modeling power, energy, covariance matrices, and Euclidean distances, to name a few examples. Consequently, a broad family of practical design problems can be represented using quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs), where both the objective function and the constraints are quadratic functions of the optimization variables. While convex QCQPs can be solved efficiently using polynomial-time algorithms, the general non-convex QCQP remains computationally challenging. Specifically, indefinite quadratic forms and rank constraints often induce NP-hardness. Non-convex QCQP problems arise in a broad range of signal processing, communications, control, machine learning, and network optimization applications.

</details>
